*Ruichi Zhang,Chikai Shang,Jiacheng Yang,Mengke Li,Yang Zhou,Junlong Gao,Yang Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CUE方法，通过引入多标签概念信号（利用CLIP提取实例级视觉线索和LLM生成类级语义线索）来缓解长尾分布下的概念混淆问题，在多个长尾基准上取得了最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长尾学习方法主要关注缓解分布偏差，忽视了长尾分布导致的概念混淆问题。该问题源于单标签监督的互斥性，抑制了相关类别间的特征共享并放大了头部类的支配地位，破坏了类间可判别性。

Method: 提出CUE（Concept-aware mUlti-label Expansion）方法：(i) 从零样本CLIP中提取实例级视觉线索；(ii) 用LLM生成类级语义线索；通过分别加权的二元逻辑调整（BLA）辅助损失整合两种线索，并与基线Logit-Adjustment（LA）损失联合优化。

Result: 在多个长尾基准数据集上，CUE方法取得了平衡且强大的性能，超越了最近的最先进方法。

Conclusion: CUE通过多标签概念信号有效恢复了长尾分布下被破坏的类间关系，验证了引入概念感知的多标签扩展是解决长尾学习中概念混淆问题的有效途径。

Abstract: Long-tailed distributions are common in real-world recognition tasks, where a few head classes have many samples while most tail classes have very few. Recently, fine-tuning foundation models for long-tailed learning has gained attention due to their excellent performance. However, most existing methods focus solely on mitigating long-tailed distribution bias while overlooking concept confusion caused by the long-tailed distribution. In this paper, we study this problem and attribute it to the mutual exclusivity of single-label supervision under long-tailed distributions, which suppresses feature sharing among related classes and amplifies the dominance of head classes, leading to disrupted inter-class discriminability. To address this, we propose CUE, Concept-aware mUlti-label Expansion, which introduces multi-label concept signals to preserve disrupted inter-class relationships. Specifically, CUE constructs concept sets by (i) extracting instance-level visual cues from zero-shot CLIP and (ii) generating class-level semantic cues with LLM; the two cues are incorporated via separately weighted Binary Logit-Adjustment (BLA) auxiliary losses and jointly optimized with the baseline Logit-Adjustment (LA) loss. Experiments on several long-tailed benchmarks, CUE achieves balanced and strong performance, surpassing recent state-of-the-art methods. Code is available at: https://github.com/zhangruichi/CUE.

</details>


### [37] [PACE: Post-Causal Entropy Modeling for Learned LiDAR Point Cloud Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01320)
*Jiahao Zhu,Kang You,Dandan Ding,Zhan Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PACE提出了一种非因果上下文聚合与轻量级可扩展预测器解耦的LiDAR点云压缩框架，在保持最先进压缩效率的同时，将自回归模式下的解码延迟降低90%以上，并支持单模型自适应不同的性能-延迟权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于八叉树结构的学习型熵模型存在两个关键瓶颈：1) 因果多阶段上下文建模导致解码延迟过高；2) 性能-延迟权衡僵化，单一模型无法适应不同约束条件。根本原因在于上下文聚合主干与概率预测紧密耦合。

Method: 将祖先上下文聚合重构为非因果主干，将因果性限制在轻量级、阶段可扩展的预测器中，消除重复的主干执行并减少计算开销。预测器支持任意数量的预测阶段，无需重载参数即可无缝适应不同的性能-延迟权衡。

Result: 在压缩效率上达到新的最先进水平，实现了显著的BD-BR节省，并将自回归模式下的解码延迟降低超过90%。

Conclusion: PACE通过解耦设计有效解决了现有方法的核心瓶颈，为LiDAR点云压缩的实际应用提供了高效且灵活的方案。

Abstract: LiDAR point cloud compression is vital for autonomous systems to handle massive data from high-resolution sensors. While learned entropy modeling built upon octree structures yields high compression gains, it faces two critical bottlenecks: 1) prohibitive latency, particularly during decoding, caused by causal, multi-stage context modeling; and 2) a rigid performance-latency trade-off, preventing a single model from adapting to varying constraints. These limitations stem from the tight coupling between context aggregation backbone and probability prediction. To address this, we propose PACE, a new framework that reformulates ancestral context aggregation as a non-causal backbone and confines causality to a lightweight, stage-scalable predictor, eliminating repetitive backbone executions and reducing computational overhead. The predictor supports an arbitrary number of prediction stages, supporting seamless adaptation across diverse performance-latency trade-offs without reloading parameters. Experiments demonstrate that PACE sets a new state-of-the-art in compression efficiency, achieving notable BD-BR savings and reducing decoding latency by over 90% in autoregressive mode, highly attractive for practical applications.

</details>


### [38] [Beyond Perceptual Shortcuts: Causal-Inspired Debiasing Optimization for Generalizable Video Reasoning in Lightweight MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01324)
*Jingze Wu,Quan Zhang,Hongfei Suo,Zeqiang Cai,Hongbo Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对轻量级多模态语言模型在强化学习微调中存在感知偏差（倾向利用数据中的感知捷径而非真正推理）的问题，本文提出VideoThinker框架，通过两阶段去偏过程（偏差感知训练+因果去偏策略优化CDPO），使轻量模型VideoThinker-R1在视频推理上达到SOTA，超越同量级和跨量级模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的RL微调方法在轻量级MLLM中效果有限，因为这些模型倾向于利用数据偏差带来的感知捷径而非发展真正的推理能力，限制了其在边缘设备部署时的应用。

Method: 提出VideoThinker框架，包含两个阶段：(1) Bias Aware Training阶段，训练一个专门的"偏差模型"来体现捷径行为；(2) Causal Debiasing Policy Optimization (CDPO)算法，通过排斥目标使主模型远离偏差模型的错误逻辑，同时拉向正确且可泛化的解决方案。

Result: VideoThinker-R1在视频推理上达到SOTA：同量级对比下，无需SFT且仅用1%训练数据，平均超越VideoRFT-3B 3.2%，在VideoMME上领先7%；跨量级对比下，在MVBench和TempCompass上分别超越Video-UTR-7B 2.1%和3.8%。

Conclusion: 通过因果启发的去偏框架，可以有效消除轻量级模型的感知偏差，显著提升其推理能力，为边缘部署场景提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Although reinforcement learning (RL) has significantly advanced reasoning capabilities in large multimodal language models (MLLMs), its efficacy remains limited for lightweight models essential for edge deployments.To address this issue, we leverage causal analysis and experiment to reveal the underlying phenomenon of perceptual bias, demonstrating that RL-based fine-tuning compels lightweight models to preferentially adopt perceptual shortcuts induced by data biases, rather than developing genuine reasoning abilities.Motivated by this insight, we propose VideoThinker, a causal-inspired framework that cultivates robust reasoning in lightweight models through a two-stage debiasing process. First, the Bias Aware Training stage forges a dedicated "bias model" to embody these shortcut behaviors. Then, the Causal Debiasing Policy Optimization (CDPO) algorithm fine-tunes the primary model, employing an innovative repulsive objective to actively push it away from the bias model's flawed logic while simultaneously pulling it toward correct, generalizable solutions.Our model, VideoThinker-R1, establishes a new state-of-the-art in video reasoning efficiency. For same-scale comparison, requiring no Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) and using only 1 of the training data for RL, it surpasses VideoRFT-3B with a 3.2% average gain on widely-used benchmarks and a 7% lead on VideoMME. For cross-scale comparison, it outperforms the larger Video-UTR-7B model on multiple benchmarks, including a 2.1% gain on MVBench and a 3.8% gain on TempCompass. Code is available at https://github.com/falonss703/VideoThinker.

</details>


### [39] [Rethinking Model Selection in VLM Through the Lens of Gromov-Wasserstein Distance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01325)
*Muyang Li,Yucheng Liu,Jianbo Ma,Elliot Osborne,Bo Han,Tongliang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了视觉编码器与VLM对齐性能之间的关系，发现常用的基于模型大小或零样本精度的选择策略与最终VLM性能仅存在弱到中等相关性。作者通过Gromov-Wasserstein距离度量跨模态结构相似性，提出了一种新的推理时指标，该指标与VLM性能强相关，能够在完整训练前有效预测VLM性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前缺乏对"什么样的视觉编码器适合VLM对齐"的原则性理解，常见的编码器选择策略（如选择最大模型或最高零样本精度）往往不能识别最优模型。

Method: 在19个预训练视觉编码器上进行系统实验，通过Gromov-Wasserstein距离度量视觉编码器与LLM之间的跨模态结构相似性，并基于此提出推理时的模型选择指标。

Result: 常用选择指标（模型大小、零样本精度）与VLM性能仅弱到中等相关；而Gromov-Wasserstein距离与VLM性能强相关，在60+完整VLM训练实验中得到验证。

Conclusion: 跨模态结构相似性是视觉编码器选择中关键但被忽视的因素，Gromov-Wasserstein距离可作为高效预测VLM训练性能的有效指标。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have enhanced traditional LLMs with visual capabilities through the integration of vision encoders. While recent works have explored various combinations of vision encoders and LLMs, there still lacks a principled understanding of what makes a vision encoder suitable for VLM alignment. In this paper, we systematically investigate this question via comprehensive experiments on a curated collection of 19 pre-trained vision encoders from diverse sources. We first demonstrate that common practices, such as choosing encoders with the largest size or highest zero-shot accuracy, consistently fail to identify optimal models. In fact, these metrics show only weak to moderate correlation with VLM performance. This intriguing finding begs a fundamental question: What factors of vision-encoders matter in VLM? Through comprehensive analysis, we identify that the structural similarity across modalities plays a crucial but previously overlooked role in vision-encoder selection, which we measure using the Gromov-Wasserstein distance as a proxy. From a theoretical perspective, we show that the learnability of cross-modality mapping can be provably associated with the Gromov-Wasserstein distance. Empirical verification on 60+ full VLM training runs shows that our proposed inference-only metric performs significantly better than alternative model selection strategies and exhibits a much stronger correlation with final VLM performance, thereby enabling efficient and effective prediction of VLM performance before full training.

</details>


### [40] [Colinearity Decay: Training Quantization-Friendly ViTs with Outlier Decay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01330)
*Jin Tong,Guang Liang,Peilin Sun,Jianxin Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Colinearity-Decay (CD)，一种针对视觉 Transformer 的结构正则化方法，通过惩罚矩阵间有害的共线性对齐来抑制激活异常值，在不改变架构或任务损失的前提下提升低比特量化部署的性能，同时保持甚至提升全精度准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有低比特量化方法要么在训练后处理量化，要么在训练中抑制大激活值，但过度限制视觉模型中的异常值会导致全精度与量化精度之间的权衡变差。作者认为训练目标应控制导致异常值有害的结构性放大，而非简单抑制异常值本身。

Method: 提出 Colinearity-Decay (CD)，一种针对 Transformer 块内有序矩阵对的结构正则化器。CD 惩罚有害的跨矩阵对齐（共线性），从而缓解极端激活值，且不改变架构或任务损失。作为解耦更新，CD 是非侵入式的，仅引入极小的训练开销。

Result: 在 ImageNet-1K 预训练、COCO 检测和下游微调任务中，CD 在多个量化流水线中一致提升了量化精度，同时保持甚至提升了全精度性能。结构正则化能有效为低比特部署准备视觉 Transformer，且零推理开销。

Conclusion: 结构正则化（CD）是比简单抑制异常值更优的策略，能够在不影响全精度性能的前提下有效提升视觉 Transformer 的低比特量化部署效果，且推理阶段无额外开销。

Abstract: Low-bit quantization is a practical route for efficiently deploying vision Transformers, yet activation outliers complicate fully quantized deployment. Existing methods either handle quantization post-training or suppress large activations during training; however, aggressively restricting outliers in vision models can lead to a poorer trade-off between full-precision and quantized accuracy. We argue that rather than simply suppressing outliers, the training objective should control the structural amplification that makes them harmful. To this end, we introduce Colinearity-Decay (CD), a structural regularizer for ordered matrix pairs within Transformer blocks. CD penalizes detrimental cross-matrix alignment and mitigates extreme activations without altering the architecture or task loss. Applied as a decoupled update, CD is non-invasive and introduces minimal training overhead. Across ImageNet-1K pre-training, COCO detection, and downstream fine-tuning, CD consistently boosts quantized accuracy across multiple pipelines while preserving, or even improving, full-precision performance. Ultimately, our results demonstrate that structural regularization effectively prepares vision Transformers for low-bit deployment with zero inference-time overhead.

</details>


### [41] [Zero-Shot Interpretable Image Steganalysis for Invertible Image Hiding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01331)
*Hao Wang,Yiming Yao,Yaguang Xie,Tong Qiao,Zhidong Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向可逆图像隐藏方案的零样本可解释图像隐写分析框架，通过融合图像隐藏、揭示和隐写分析，并采用残差增强策略，在跨数据集和跨架构场景下显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有隐写分析方法仅做二分类（隐写图/封面图），且要求训练和测试数据分布相似，难以应用于真实场景；针对可逆图像隐藏方案的隐写分析研究不足。

Method: 1）将图像隐藏、揭示和隐写分析集成到统一框架中，使隐写分析组件具备恢复秘密信息的能力；2）设计残差增强策略生成隐写图像，提升隐写分析器在跨数据集和跨架构场景下的泛化能力。

Result: 在基准数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法在可逆图像隐藏方案的隐写分析任务上显著优于现有技术。

Conclusion: 所提出的可解释零样本隐写分析框架有效克服了传统方法的局限性，兼具可解释性和泛化能力，适用于更真实的场景。

Abstract: Image steganalysis, which aims at detecting secret information concealed within images, has become a critical countermeasure for assessing the security of steganography methods, especially the emerging invertible image hiding approaches. However, prior studies merely classify input images into two categories (i.e., stego or cover) and typically conduct steganalysis under the constraint that training and testing data must follow similar distribution, thereby hindering their application in real-world scenarios. To overcome these shortcomings, we propose a novel interpretable image steganalysis framework tailored for invertible image hiding schemes under a challenging zero-shot setting. Specifically, we integrate image hiding, revealing, and steganalysis into a unified framework, endowing the steganalysis component with the capability to recover the secret information embedded in stego images. Additionally, we elaborate a simple yet effective residual augmentation strategy for generating stego images to further enhance the generalizability of the steganalyzer in cross-dataset and cross-architecture scenarios. Extensive experiments on benchmark datasets demonstrate that our proposed approach significantly outperforms the existing steganalysis techniques for invertible image hiding schemes.

</details>


### [42] [Active Reasoning Vision-Language Models via Sequential Experimental Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01345)
*Anjie Liu,Ziqin Gong,Yan Song,Yuxiang Chen,Xiaolong Liu,Hengtong Lu,Kaike Zhang,Chen Wei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出将视觉感知中的带宽瓶颈问题形式化为序贯贝叶斯最优实验设计（S-BOED）问题，并通过无需训练的推理策略，在千兆像素级别上平衡空间覆盖与分辨率，显著提升了现有视觉-语言模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代视觉-语言模型（VLMs）存在感知带宽瓶颈：宽视野必然牺牲细粒度细节，限制了复杂推理能力。受主动视觉和信息觅食范式启发，作者希望克服这一局限性。

Method: 将问题形式化为序贯贝叶斯最优实验设计（S-BOED）问题，在连续千兆像素空间中推导出可处理的近似解以平衡空间覆盖与分辨率；提出一种无需训练的推理策略作为S-BOED目标的实用实例化，支持从贪心采样到前瞻规划等多种优化算法。

Result: 在千兆像素级基准测试上，该方法进一步提升了最先进模型的性能，显著优于标准基线，并有效缩小了与人工标注基准的差距。

Conclusion: 通过贝叶斯最优实验设计的框架，可以在无需额外训练的情况下，通过序贯决策有效平衡视野广度与细节精度，提升VLMs在复杂视觉推理任务上的表现。

Abstract: Visual perception in modern Vision-Language Models (VLMs) is constrained by a fundamental perceptual bandwidth bottleneck: a broad field of view inevitably sacrifices the fine-grained details necessary for complex reasoning. Inspired by the classical paradigms of active vision and information foraging, we frame overcoming this limitation as a sequential decision-making process. We formalise this process through the lens of the sequential Bayesian optimal experimental design (S-BOED) problem. While exact Bayesian inference is intractable in continuous gigapixel spaces, we derive principled yet tractable approximations that balance spatial coverage against resolution. To validate this framework, we present a training-free inference strategy as a practical instantiation of the S-BOED objective for agents equipped with multiple vision tools. Designed as a flexible template, this strategy accommodates arbitrary optimisation algorithms, ranging from efficient greedy sampling to look-ahead planning, to approximate the optimal design. Empirical evaluations on gigapixel-level benchmarks demonstrate that our approach further boosts the performance of state-of-the-art models, significantly outperforming standard baselines and effectively narrowing the gap towards human-annotated oracles.

</details>


### [43] [CHASE: Competing Hypotheses for Ambiguity-Aware Selective Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01346)
*Kartik Jhawar,Yuhao Geng,Atul N. Parikh,Lipo Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CHASE是一个通过显式比较结构化时间解释（竞争假设）来进行选择性预测的框架，在部分可观测条件下优于传统不确定性估计方法。在隐藏连接推断任务中，相比基线方法，CHASE在80%覆盖率下实现了11.0%的对齐度提升和8.8%的三路准确率提升，并在90%覆盖率下降低了9.9%的整体风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统选择性预测方法依赖单一预测分支的输出进行不确定性估计，在部分可观测条件下（局部时序证据矛盾）表现不佳，标准置信度分数具有误导性。

Method: 提出CHASE框架，通过显式比较结构化时间解释（竞争假设）来确定是做出决策还是弃权。当真正歧义导致竞争假设之间的分数差距缩小时，CHASE优化一个排序感知的选择器，基于这些假设边际来全局区分安全承诺与根本性不确定的情况。

Result: 在80%覆盖率下，相比基线方法，CHASE在整体对齐度上获得11.0%的相对平均提升，在极高歧义区域三路准确率提升8.8%，整体风险降低9.9%（90%覆盖率下）。零样本定性迁移到真实GUV视频也证明了有效性。

Conclusion: 显式推理竞争假设在选择性预测中提供了更优的指标平衡，尤其是在结构歧义场景下，为部分可观测条件下的决策提供了更可靠的方法。

Abstract: Standard selective prediction methods typically estimate uncertainty from the output of a single predictive branch. While effective for general uncertainty estimation, these approaches often struggle under partial observability, where local temporal evidence can be contradictory and standard confidence scores become misleading. We introduce CHASE (Competing Hypotheses for Ambiguity-Aware Selective Prediction), a selective prediction framework that explicitly compares structured temporal explanations to determine whether to commit to a decision or abstain. Because genuine ambiguity causes the score gap between competing hypotheses to collapse, CHASE optimizes a ranking-aware selector over these hypothesis margins to globally separate safe commitments from fundamentally uncertain ones. We evaluate this framework on the problem of hidden connectivity inference, utilizing a controlled, physically grounded simulator inspired by the dynamics of giant unilamellar vesicles (GUVs), alongside zero-shot qualitative transfer (without retraining or fine tuning) to representative real GUV videos. Our experiments demonstrate that explicitly reasoning over competing hypotheses provides a superior balance of metrics. Compared to canonical uncertainty baselines, CHASE achieves statistically significant gains in overall no-abstain accuracy, three-way accuracy, and overall ambiguity-aligned abstention (at 80% coverage). Specifically, it yields up to an 11.0% relative mean improvement in overall alignment, alongside up to an 8.8% relative boost in three-way accuracy in the very-high ambiguity regime. By maintaining a selective risk boundary strictly at par with the best baselines at 80% coverage, and reducing overall risk by 9.9% at 90% coverage, this framework offers a more reliable approach to decision-making under structured ambiguity.

</details>


### [44] [AgriKD: Cross-Architecture Knowledge Distillation for Efficient Leaf Disease Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01355)
*Minh-Dung Le,Minh-Duc Hoang,Hoang-Vu Truong,Thi-Thu-Hong Phan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AgriKD，一种跨架构知识蒸馏框架，将Vision Transformer (ViT)教师模型的知识蒸馏到轻量级卷积学生模型，实现高效边缘部署。通过输出级、特征级和关系级多目标蒸馏，弥合Transformer与CNN间的表征差异。实验表明，学生模型在性能接近教师的同时，参数量减少约172倍，计算量减少47.57倍，推理延迟降低18-22倍，并在NVIDIA Jetson和移动应用上实现实时推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ViT在叶片疾病分类中表现出色，但其高计算成本使其无法在资源受限的边缘设备上部署；现有方法难以将ViT的丰富表征有效迁移到轻量级模型中。

Method: 提出AgriKD跨架构知识蒸馏框架，以ViT为教师、紧凑CNN为学生，集成输出级、特征级和关系级多目标蒸馏损失，弥合Transformer与CNN间的表征鸿沟。

Result: 学生模型性能接近教师，参数量减少约172倍，计算量减少47.57倍，推理延迟降低18-22倍；在ONNX、TFLite Float16、TensorRT FP16多种运行时格式下保持一致性能，并在Jetson边缘设备和移动应用中实现可靠实时推理。

Conclusion: AgriKD成功将ViT的全局表征能力迁移至轻量CNN，为资源受限环境下的AI农业应用提供了实用的边缘部署方案。

Abstract: Automated leaf disease classification is critical for early disease detection in resource-constrained field environments. Vision Transformers (ViTs) provide strong representation capability by modeling long-range dependencies and inter-class relationships; however, their high computational cost makes them impractical for deployment on edge devices. As a result, existing approaches struggle to effectively transfer these rich representations to lightweight models. This paper introduces AgriKD, a cross-architecture knowledge distillation framework for efficient edge deployment, which transfers knowledge from a Vision Transformer (ViT) teacher to a compact convolutional student model. To bridge the representational gap between Transformer and CNN architectures, the proposed approach integrates multiple distillation objectives at the output, feature, and relational levels, where each objective captures a different aspect of the teacher knowledge. This enables the student model to better preserve and utilize transformer-derived global representations. Experiments on multiple leaf disease datasets show that the distilled student achieves performance comparable to the teacher while significantly improving efficiency, reducing model parameters by approximately 172 times, computational cost by 47.57 times, and inference latency by 18-22 times. Furthermore, the optimized model is deployed across multiple runtime formats, including ONNX, TFLite Float16, and TensorRT FP16, achieving consistent predictive performance with negligible accuracy degradation. Real-world deployment on NVIDIA Jetson edge devices and a mobile application demonstrates reliable real-time inference, highlighting the practicality of AgriKD for AI-powered agricultural applications in resource-constrained environments.

</details>


### [45] [VoxAfford: Multi-Scale Voxel-Token Fusion for Open-Vocabulary 3D Affordance Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01365)
*Haowen Sun,Shaolong Zhang,Mingyang Li,Chengzhong Ma,Xinzhe Chen,Qiongjie Cui,Xingyu Chen,Zeyang Liu,Xuguang Lan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VoxAfford通过向MLLM的输出令牌注入来自预训练3D VQVAE的多尺度几何特征，并使用交叉注意力机制让语义查询匹配几何模式，再通过兼容门控控制注入强度，最终聚合为空间感知的affordance提示以生成分割掩码，在开放词汇3D affordance检测任务上mIoU提升约8%，并实现了零样本迁移到真实机器人场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于MLLM的方法使用自回归生成的特殊输出令牌解码为分割掩码，但这些令牌仅建模序列依赖关系而非空间邻域关系，导致语义丰富但空间信息贫乏，难以精确定位3D交互区域。

Method: 1) 将冻结的预训练3D VQVAE编码器的多尺度几何特征注入到MLLM输出令牌中；2) 每个输出令牌以affordance语义为查询，通过交叉注意力从对应体素尺度检索几何模式，并用可学习兼容门控控制注入强度；3) 增强后的令牌通过语义条件注意力聚合成空间感知的affordance提示，与逐点特征共同传播生成最终掩码。

Result: 在开放词汇affordance检测任务中，mIoU提升约8%，达到最先进水平；真实机器人实验验证了零样本迁移到新物体的能力。

Conclusion: VoxAfford通过向MLLM输出令牌注入多尺度几何特征，有效弥补了自回归生成导致的空间信息不足问题，实现了语义与几何的融合，显著提升了开放词汇3D affordance检测的性能。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary 3D affordance detection requires localizing interaction regions on point clouds given novel affordance descriptions. Recent methods extend multimodal large language models (MLLMs) with special output tokens that are decoded into segmentation masks. However, these tokens are produced through autoregressive generation, which models sequential dependencies rather than spatial neighborhood relations, leaving them semantically rich but spatially impoverished for 3D localization. We propose Voxel-enhanced Affordance detection (VoxAfford), which bypasses this bottleneck by injecting multi-scale geometric features from a frozen pre-trained 3D VQVAE encoder into the output tokens after generation. Each output token uses its affordance semantics as a query to retrieve relevant geometric patterns from its paired voxel scale via cross-attention, with a learned compatibility gate controlling the injection strength. The enhanced tokens are then aggregated into a spatially-aware affordance prompt through semantic-conditioned attention and propagated alongside per-point features to generate the final mask. Experiments on open-vocabulary affordance detection tasks show that VoxAfford achieves state-of-the-art performance with approximately an 8% improvement in mIoU, and real robot experiments confirm zero-shot transfer to novel objects.

</details>


### [46] [VISTA: Video Interaction Spatio-Temporal Analysis Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01391)
*Alejandro Aparcedo,Akash Kumar,Aaryan Garg,Dalton Pham,Wen-Kai Chen,Anirudh Bharadwaj,Aman Chadha,Yogesh Rawat*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VISTA是一个面向视觉语言模型(VLM)的大规模交互感知诊断基准，通过构建包含多实体、多动作的开集时空理解数据集（约1.2万视频-查询对）及分类学诊断框架，系统评估了11个SOTA VLM在关系、空间和时间维度上的表现，揭示了传统指标无法捕捉的时空偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM基准仅评估简单单动作视频、封闭属性集和受限实体类型，无法反映真实世界中多样实体间的自由多动作交互；同时缺乏系统化的故障分析框架来跨互补时空轴评估模型。

Method: 提出VISTA基准，将视频分解为可解释实体、关联动作和关系动态，实现多轴诊断；整合多个数据集形成交互感知分类学，构建约1.2万视频-查询对，涵盖多样场景和复杂度。

Result: 系统评估了11个SOTA VLM，通过分类学驱动的细粒度性能分解，揭示了传统聚合指标掩盖的模型缺陷和显著时空偏差。

Conclusion: VISTA是首个大规模交互感知的VLM时空理解诊断基准，为模型设计、预训练策略和评估协议提供细粒度指导框架。

Abstract: Existing benchmarks for Vision-Language Models (VLMs) primarily evaluate spatio-temporal understanding on simple single-action videos, closed attribute sets and restricted entity types, failing to capture the freeform, multi-action interactions between diverse entities which characterize real-world video understanding. Furthermore, the lack of a systematic framework for analyzing model failures across complementary spatio-temporal axes hinders comprehensive evaluation. To address these gaps, we introduce VISTA, a Video Interaction Spatio-Temporal Analysis benchmark designed for open-set, multi-entity and multi-action spatio-temporal understanding in VLMs. VISTA decomposes videos into interpretable entities, their associated actions, and relational dynamics, enabling multi-axis diagnostics and unified assessment of relational, spatial, and temporal understanding. Our benchmark integrates multiple datasets into a single interaction-aware taxonomy and comprises ~12K curated video-query pairs spanning diverse scenes and complexities. We systematically evaluate 11 state-of-the-art VLMs on VISTA, and break down aggregate performance across our taxonomy to reveal shortcomings and pronounced spatio-temporal biases obscured by traditional metrics. By providing detailed, taxonomy-driven diagnostics on a challenging dataset, VISTA offers a nuanced framework to guide advances in model design, pretraining strategies, and evaluation protocols. Overall, VISTA is the first, large-scale, interaction-aware diagnostic benchmark for spatio-temporal understanding in VLMs.

</details>


### [47] [Recall to Predict: Grounding Motion Forecasting in Interpretable Motion Bank](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01393)
*Abhishek Vivekanandan,Ahmed Abouelazm,J. Marius Zöllner*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Recall2Predict框架，通过对比学习构建"运动库"结构化的可行驶轨迹嵌入空间，利用锚点检索层动态检索显式运动先验，结合双级门控交叉注意力、Straight-Through Gumbel-Softmax离散轨迹选择和DETR风格解码器进行几何精炼，在Argoverse 2和Waymo Open Motion数据集上取得了竞争性的多模态运动预测精度，同时消除了标准潜在查询的"黑箱"问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有运动预测方法需要在可解释性和预测精度之间权衡：基于锚点的架构依赖不透明的潜在查询易导致潜在崩溃，而简单的轨迹采样限制了多模态多样性。

Method: 1) 通过对比学习构建"运动库"结构化的可行驶轨迹嵌入空间；2) 提出锚点检索层，使用正交初始化的查询配合双级门控交叉注意力机制动态检索显式运动先验；3) 采用Straight-Through Gumbel-Softmax估计器实现离散轨迹选择的端到端可微分优化；4) DETR风格解码器进行几何精炼；5) 联合优化Winner-Takes-All运动学高斯混合模型、潜在多样性惩罚和soft-min加权终点损失。

Result: 在Argoverse 2和Waymo Open Motion数据集上取得了竞争性的多模态预测精度，同时通过将解码阶段严格条件化在多样、可解释的运动基元上，消除了标准潜在查询的"黑箱"问题。

Conclusion: 通过显式、可解释的运动基元来条件化解码阶段，可以在保持竞争性多模态精度的同时，消除传统潜在查询的不透明性，实现可解释性与预测精度的有效平衡。

Abstract: Motion forecasting often requires trading interpretability for predictive accuracy. Standard anchor-based architectures rely on opaque latent queries that are highly prone to latent collapse, or naive trajectory sampling that limits multi-modal diversity. We propose an end-to-end differentiable framework that grounds predictions in a comprehensive "motion bank", a structured embedding space of physically realizable trajectories constructed via contrastive learning. Rather than regressing paths from a blank slate, our architecture dynamically retrieves explicit motion priors using a novel Anchor Retrieval Layer. This module adapts orthogonally initialized queries via a Dual-Level Gated Cross-Attention mechanism and executes discrete trajectory selection using a Straight-Through Gumbel-Softmax estimator to preserve continuous gradient flow. The retrieved semantically grounded anchors are then geometrically refined by a DETR-style decoder, optimized jointly with a Winner-Takes-All (WTA) kinematic Gaussian Mixture Model (GMM), a latent diversity penalty, and a soft-min weighted endpoint loss. By strictly conditioning the decoding phase on diverse, interpretable motion primitives, our approach eliminates the "black box" of standard latent queries while achieving competitive multi-modal accuracy on the Argoverse 2 and Waymo Open Motion datasets. Code is available at: https://github.com/abviv/recall2predict

</details>


### [48] [SRGAN-CKAN: Expressive Super-Resolution with Nonlinear Functional Operators under Minimal Resources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01459)
*Roberto Isai Navaro-Aviña,Eduardo Said Merin-Martinez,Andres Mendez-Vazquez,Eduardo Rodriguez-Tello*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SRGAN--CKAN框架，通过在对抗学习中将卷积替换为基于样条函数的非线性变换（Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks），在有限计算资源下提升单图像超分辨率的局部表达能力，实现了感知质量与重建保真度的良好平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SISR方法中，基于Transformer和扩散模型的方法虽提升了全局建模和感知质量，但计算复杂度过高。本文旨在不依赖大规模计算资源的前提下，增强局部算子的表达能力。

Method: 提出SRGAN--CKAN混合超分辨率框架，将卷积Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks（CKAN）集成到对抗学习框架中，用基于样条函数的非线性局部变换替代传统线性卷积映射，实现表达性更强的局部建模。

Result: 在受限计算条件下，该方法在提升感知质量的同时保持了重建保真度，在失真指标和感知指标之间取得了有利平衡，展现了高效性。

Conclusion: 本文为现有方法提供了补充方向，通过提升局部变换的表示能力，提供了一种高效、可扩展的替代方案，无需依赖全局密集架构。

Abstract: Single-Image Super-Resolution (SISR) aims to reconstruct a High-Resolution (HR) image from a Low-Resolution (LR) observation, a fundamentally ill-posed problem where high-frequency details are severely degraded at large upscaling factors. Recent advances have been driven by transformer-based architectures and diffusion models improve global context modeling and perceptual quality at the cost of increased computational complexity. In contrast, this work focuses on enhancing the expressivity of local operators under minimal resources. We propose SRGAN--CKAN, a hybrid super-resolution framework that integrates Convolutional Kolmogorov--Arnold Networks (CKAN) into an adversarial learning setting reformulating convolution as a nonlinear patch-based transformation. The proposed operator replaces linear local mappings with spline-based functional representations, allowing expressive modeling of complex local structures and high-frequency textures using minimal hardware resources. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed approach improves perceptual quality while preserving reconstruction fidelity, achieving a favorable balance between distortion-based and perceptual metrics. These results are obtained under constrained computational settings, highlighting the efficiency of the proposed formulation. Overall, this work introduces a complementary direction to existing approaches by improving the representational power of local transformations, providing an efficient and scalable alternative to globally intensive architectures.

</details>


### [49] [SplAttN: Bridging 2D and 3D with Gaussian Soft Splatting and Attention for Point Cloud Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01466)
*Zhaoyang Li,Zhichao You,Tianrui Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对多模态点云补全中硬投影导致跨模态熵坍塌（稀疏支持阻隔视觉先验传播）的问题，提出可微高斯泼溅（Differentiable Gaussian Splatting）替代硬投影，实现密集连续图像平面表征，增强跨模态连接可学习性，在PCN、ShapeNet-55/34和KITTI上达到SOTA，并通过反事实评估验证了模型对视觉线索的鲁棒依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态点云补全的理论机制尚不清晰，现有工作归因于模态间连接，但标准硬投影将稀疏点云投影到图像平面会产生极度稀疏的支持区域，阻碍视觉先验传播，即跨模态熵坍塌问题。

Method: 提出SplAttN，用可微高斯泼溅（Differentiable Gaussian Splatting）替代硬投影，将投影过程重构为连续密度估计，生成密集连续的图像平面表示，避免稀疏支持坍塌、促进梯度流动并增强跨模态连接的可学习性。

Result: 在PCN和ShapeNet-55/34上达到SOTA性能；在KITTI真实场景反事实评估中，基线方法退化为不依赖视觉线索的单模态模板检索器，而SplAttN保持对视觉线索的鲁棒依赖。

Conclusion: SplAttN通过可微高斯泼溅有效建立了跨模态连接，解决了硬投影导致的熵坍塌问题，在多模态点云补全中实现了更鲁棒的视觉先验利用。

Abstract: Although multi-modal learning has advanced point cloud completion, the theoretical mechanisms remain unclear. Recent works attribute success to the connection between modalities, yet we identify that standard hard projection severs this connection: projecting a sparse point cloud onto the image plane yields an extremely sparse support, which hinders visual prior propagation, a failure mode we term Cross-Modal Entropy Collapse. To address this practical limitation, we propose SplAttN, which replaces hard projection with Differentiable Gaussian Splatting to produce a dense, continuous image-plane representation. By reformulating projection as continuous density estimation, SplAttN avoids collapsed sparse support, facilitates gradient flow, and improves cross-modal connection learnability. Extensive experiments show that SplAttN achieves state-of-the-art performance on PCN and ShapeNet-55/34. Crucially, we utilize the real-world KITTI benchmark as a stress test for multi-modal reliance. Counter-factual evaluation reveals that while baselines degenerate into unimodal template retrievers insensitive to visual removal, SplAttN maintains a robust dependency on visual cues, validating that our method establishes an effective cross-modal connection. Code is available at https://github.com/zay002/SplAttN.

</details>


### [50] [Decision Boundary-aware Generation for Long-tailed Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01468)
*Jiacheng Yang,Ruichi Zhang,Chikai Shang,Mengke Li,Xinyi Shang,Junlong Gao,Yonggang Zhang,Yang Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出决策边界感知生成（DBG）框架，针对扩散模型生成增强中头到尾迁移导致的边界模糊问题，通过生成信息性近边界样本来提升长尾学习中的决策空间可分离性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长尾数据导致分类器偏向头部类、尾部类准确率下降。扩散生成增强虽可缓解，但头到尾迁移引入非局部特征混合，造成类间特征纠缠、决策边界重叠和尾部类分布偏移。

Method: 提出决策边界感知生成（DBG）框架，通过生成信息性近边界样本促进近边界表示学习，从而在长尾数据上重平衡的同时获得更可分离的决策空间。

Result: 在标准长尾基准上，DBG一致提升尾部类和总体准确率，且类间重叠更少。

Conclusion: DBG通过针对性生成近边界样本，有效解决了头到尾迁移导致的边界模糊问题，实现了更优的长尾学习性能。

Abstract: Long-tailed data bias decision boundaries toward head classes and degrade tail class accuracy. Diffusion-based generative augmentation address this problem by generating additional data, while head-to-tail transfer further mitigate the generator bias inherit from long-tailed dataset. However, we show that while head-to-tail transfer helps balance the decision space of the classifier, it also induces latent non-local feature mixing that entangles inter-class features, causing decision boundary overlap and tail class distribution shift. To address this, we first identify the problem of boundary ambiguity and then propose Decision Boundary-aware Generation (DBG) framework, which promotes near-boundary representation learning by generating informative near-boundary samples. Overall, DBG rebalances the long-tailed dataset while yielding more separable decision space for long-tailed learning. Across standard long-tailed benchmarks, DBG consistently improves tail class and overall accuracy with less inter-class overlap. The code of DBG is available at https://github.com/keepdigitalabc-svg/DBG.

</details>


### [51] [CSGuard: Toward Forgery-Resistant Watermarking in Diffusion Models via Compressed Sensing Constraint](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01479)
*Jiewei Lai,Lan Zhang,Chen Tang,Pengcheng Sun,Zhaopeng Zhang,Yunhao Wang,Hui Jin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CSGuard，一种基于压缩感知的抗伪造扩散模型水印方案，通过秘密矩阵绑定生成与验证过程，将伪造攻击成功率从100%降至28.12%，同时保持100%的良性水印检测率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于潜空间的扩散模型水印方法存在严重伪造漏洞：攻击者可通过逆向水印图像并重新生成任意内容，实现恶意内容的虚假归属。

Method: 利用压缩感知理论，将水印图像的生成和验证过程绑定到一个秘密矩阵上，只有持有该秘密矩阵的用户才能正确嵌入或验证水印。

Result: 攻击成功率从100%降至28.12%，良性水印图像检测率达100%，且不损害生成质量与水印完整性。

Conclusion: CSGuard是首个抗伪造的水印方案，在不牺牲水印有效性的前提下显著提升了对伪造攻击的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Latent-based diffusion model watermarking embeds watermarks into generated images' latent space to enable content attribution, offering a training-free solution for intellectual property protection and digital forensics. However, these methods exhibit a critical vulnerability to the forgery attack, attackers can extract the watermark by inverting the watermarked image and re-generating it with an arbitrary prompt, thereby enabling false attribution on malicious content. In this paper, we propose the CSGuard, the first forgery-resistant watermarking schema that leverages compressed sensing to bind the watermarked image generation and verification to a secret matrix. This ensures that only users possessing the secret matrix can correctly embed or verify the image watermark, prevents the illegal users from forgery without compromising generation quality and watermark integrity. Experimental results demonstrate that CSGuard achieves strong forgery resistance, reduces the attack success rate from 100.0\% to 28.12\%, and achieve 100\% detection rate on benign watermarked images without compromising watermarking effectiveness.

</details>


### [52] [AttnRouter: Per-Category Attention Routing for Training-Free Image Editing on MMDiT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01480)
*Guandong Li,Mengxia Ye*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对Qwen-Image-Edit-2511（60块多模态扩散Transformer）研究无需训练的图像编辑方法。提出KVInject（单前向注意力操作，在局部层/步带中将源图像K/V投影alpha混合到噪声半部），比经典MasaCtrl更简单且避免了提示不匹配故障；提出AttnRouter（按编辑类型分派的注意力操作路由表），将CLIP-T+DINO-I复合分数提升6.4%；通过消融实验定位了编辑有效的注意力子电路：早期去噪步（S0-7）的K/V注入几乎恢复全部增益，alpha在[0.3,0.5]为稳定区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在MMDiT架构上实现无需训练的图像编辑，解决经典MasaCtrl方法在多模态扩散Transformer上的提示不匹配失效问题，并系统研究不同注意力操作对各类编辑任务的影响。

Method: (i) KVInject：单前向注意力操作，在选定的层和步带内，将源图像部分的K/V投影以alpha权重混合到噪声半部；(ii) AttnRouter：基于编辑类别的注意力操作路由表，自动分派最优操作；(iii) 系统性消融实验定位有效子电路。

Result: KVInject避免MasaCtrl的提示不匹配故障（MasaCtrl复合分数下降31%）；AttnRouter提升复合分数6.4%；自动CLIP零样本分类器弥补了98%的差距；早期去噪步（S0-7）的K/V注入恢复近全部增益；alpha在[0.3,0.5]为稳定区间；简单K/V缩放从未超越基线。

Conclusion: 提出了一套适用于MMDiT架构的无训练图像编辑方案，揭示了该架构下编辑有效的注意力子电路特性，证明了UNet时代的经验不能直接迁移到MMDiT。

Abstract: We study training-free image editing on Qwen-Image-Edit-2511, a 60-block multi-modal diffusion transformer (MMDiT) that concatenates noise and source-image tokens within a single attention stream. We make three contributions. (i) We introduce KVInject, a single-forward attention manipulation that alpha-blends source-half key/value projections into the noise-half within a localized layer/step band. KVInject is simpler than the classical two-pass MasaCtrl recipe and avoids the prompt-mismatch failure mode that disables MasaCtrl on MMDiT (composite score drops 31% versus baseline). (ii) We show that no single attention operation dominates across edit types, motivating AttnRouter, a per-category routing table that dispatches edits to the operation that best preserves source structure for that type. With ground-truth categories the router improves the CLIP-T+DINO-I composite by 6.4% over the editing baseline; an automatic CLIP zero-shot classifier closes 98% of this gap despite only 55% category accuracy. (iii) Through layer-, step-, and alpha-band ablations we localize the editing-effective attention sub-circuit: K/V injection in early denoising steps (S0-7) recovers nearly all of the gain of full-step injection, while injection in early (L0-15) or late (L45-60) layer bands fails to drive editing entirely; alpha in [0.3, 0.5] is a stable sweet spot. We also report negative results that highlight what does not transfer from the UNet folklore: simple K/V rescaling never beats baseline and aggressive variants collapse generation entirely (composite 0.084). We release code, pre-computed routing tables, and a 100-sample stratified subset of ImgEdit-Bench used in all ablations.

</details>


### [53] [CGFformer: Cluster-Guidance Frequency Transformer for Pansharpening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01490)
*Zijian Zhou,Jianing Zhang,Kai Sun,Xiangyu Zhao,Chunxia Zhang,Xiangyong Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CGFformer，一种基于聚类引导的频率Transformer方法，通过自适应频率分离、双流去噪和频率-空间融合模块，在全色锐化任务中显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于频率的全色锐化方法使用固定频率滤波器，无法精确适应复杂的空间变化频率分布；且去噪策略对频率成分利用不足，难以有效抑制多种噪声类型。

Method: 1) 自适应分离模块：通过K-means聚类融合局部特征与非局部信息，实现高低频成分精确分离；2) 双流精炼模块：结合Transformer交叉注意力去除多种噪声，联合抑制频率相关与无关干扰；3) 频率-空间融合模块：增强细节并促进空间-频率交互，有效重建空间结构。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，CGFformer相比现有全色锐化方法取得了显著改进。

Conclusion: CGFformer通过聚类引导的频率Transformer有效解决了固定频率滤波和噪声抑制不足的问题，为全色锐化提供了更优的方案。

Abstract: Pansharpening aims to generate high-resolution multispectral (HRMS) images by fusing low-resolution multispectral (LRMS) images with high-resolution panchromatic (PAN) images. However, the current mainstream frequency-based pansharpening methods employ fixed frequency filters, which cannot precisely adapt to complex and spatially diversified frequency distributions in PAN and MS images. Furthermore, existing denoising strategies insufficiently exploit frequency components for denoising and struggle to suppress various noise types accurately. To address these challenges, we propose CGFformer, a cluster-guidance frequency Transformer that focuses on varying frequency distribution and interactions between frequency and spatial components. Specifically, we design an adaptive separation module that integrates local features and non-local information through K-means clustering, enabling more precise separation of high- and low-frequency components. Subsequently, we introduce a dual-stream refinement module combined with Transformer-based cross-attention to remove various noise, allowing the network to jointly suppress frequency-relevant and irrelevant disturbances. In addition, we develop a frequency-spatial fusion module designed to enhance detail and facilitate spatial-frequency interaction, ensuring more effective reconstruction of spatial structures in the fused results. Extensive experiments on multiple benchmark datasets demonstrate that the proposed CGFformer achieves notable improvements over existing pansharpening approaches.

</details>


### [54] [SF20K Competition 2025: Summary and findings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01496)
*Ridouane Ghermi,Xi Wang,Vicky Kalogeiton,Ivan Laptev*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了第一届SF20K竞赛（与ICCV 2025 SLoMO研讨会联办），旨在推动故事级别视频理解，超越传统短片段动作识别。竞赛基于业余短片语料库构建开放式视频问答任务，包含Main Track（无限制）和Special Track（<8B参数）两个赛道，共22支队伍286次提交。最佳成绩Main Track 65.7%，Special Track 48.7%，远低于人类表现91.7%。关键发现：叙事感知的镜头级处理优于均匀帧采样；精心设计的多阶段小模型管线可匹敌或超越30倍以上大模型的端到端推理；字幕质量是性能的主导因素。核心瓶颈在于信息选择与推理结构，而非模型容量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频理解研究多聚焦于短片段动作识别，缺乏对完整故事级别叙事理解的评估基准与竞赛，尤其是开放式问答形式。作者希望推动模型从记忆流行电影转向真正的多模态叙事理解。

Method: 构建SF20K-Test基准（95部短片，979个问答对），采用LLM-QA-Eval（基于GPT-4.1-nano的自动评判器）。设置Main Track（无模型大小限制）和Special Track（<8B参数）。竞赛共22支队伍286次提交。

Result: Main Track最佳65.7%准确率，Special Track最佳48.7%，人类表现91.7%。叙事感知的镜头级处理优于均匀帧采样；多阶段小模型管线可匹配或超越大模型；字幕质量是关键决定因素。

Conclusion: 长视频问答的主要瓶颈在于信息选择与推理结构，而非原始模型容量；当前方法与人类级别的叙事理解之间仍存在巨大差距。

Abstract: This report presents the results and findings of the first edition of the Short-Films 20K (SF20K) Competition, held in conjunction with the SLoMO Workshop at ICCV 2025. The competition is designed to advance story-level video understanding beyond short-clip action recognition, introducing an open-ended video question-answering task built on a corpus of amateur short films. This setup ensures that models must rely on multimodal understanding rather than memorization of popular movies. Evaluation is conducted using the SF20K-Test benchmark (95 movies, 979 question-answer pairs) and scored via LLM-QA-Eval, an automated judge based on GPT-4.1-nano. The competition attracted 22 teams and 286 submissions across two tracks: a Main Track with unrestricted model size and a Special Track limited to models under 8 billion parameters. The winning team achieved 65.7% accuracy on the Main Track and 48.7% on the Special Track, against a human performance ceiling of 91.7%. Our analysis reveals several key findings: narrative-aware, shot-level processing consistently outperforms uniform frame sampling; well-designed multi-stage pipelines using smaller models can match or exceed end-to-end inference with models over 30x larger; and subtitle quality is a dominant factor in performance. These results highlight that the primary bottleneck in long-form video QA lies in information selection and reasoning structure rather than raw model capacity, and that a substantial gap remains between current methods and human-level narrative comprehension.

</details>


### [55] [Towards Visual Query Localization in the 3D World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01498)
*Liang Peng,Bohan Tan,Zhipeng Zhang,Haobo Li,Yifan Jiao,Xingping Dong,Libo Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次提出3D视觉查询定位基准3DVQL，包含多模态数据（点云、RGB、深度图），并提出了LaF融合算法，显著优于现有基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉查询定位研究主要集中在2D视频领域，3D空间的对应问题缺乏关注，缺乏相应的基准数据集和评估方法。

Method: 构建了3DVQL基准数据集（2002个序列、约17万帧、6.4K响应轨迹段、38个类别），提供点云、RGB、深度图多模态数据，并设计了Lift-and-Attention Fusion (LaF)算法。

Result: 现有方法在不同融合模块上表现出显著性能差异，LaF算法在各项指标上显著优于已有基线模型。

Conclusion: 3DVQL填补了3D多模态视觉查询定位领域的空白，LaF融合算法为后续研究提供了有效基线，数据集和代码将开源。

Abstract: Visual query localization (VQL) aims to predict the spatio-temporal response of the most recent occurrence in a sequence given a query. Currently, most research focuses on visual query localization in 2D videos, while its counterpart in 3D space has received little attention. In this paper, we make the first attempt to address visual query localization in the 3D world by introducing a novel benchmark, dubbed 3DVQL. Specifically, 3DVQL contains 2,002 sequences with around 170,000 frames and 6.4K response track segments from 38 object categories. Each sequence in 3DVQL is provided with multiple modalities, including point clouds, RGB images, and depth images, to support flexible research. To ensure high-quality annotations, each sequence is manually annotated with multiple rounds of verification and refinement. To the best of our knowledge, 3DVQL is the first benchmark for 3D multimodal visual query localization. To facilitate comparison in subsequent research, we implement a series of representative 3D multimodal VQL baselines using point clouds and RGB images. The experimental results show that existing methods exhibit significant performance variations across different fusion modules. To encourage future research, we propose a lift-and-attention fusion algorithm named LaF, which significantly outperforms existing baseline models. Our benchmark and model will be publicly released at https://github.com/wuhengliangliang/3DVQL.

</details>


### [56] [SwiftPie: Lightning-fast Subject-driven Image Personalization via One step Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01510)
*Huy Duong,Trong-Tung Nguyen,Cuong Pham,Anh Tran,Khoi Nguyen,Minh Hoai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SwiftPie是首个无需多步迭代、仅需一步扩散即可完成主体驱动图像个性化生成的方法，通过双分支身份注入机制与掩码引导的重缩放策略，在媲美多步方法的身份保真度和提示对齐性能下，实现了闪电般的生成速度，为实时、交互式个性化图像合成开辟了可能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散模型的图像个性化方法依赖计算密集的微调、迭代优化或多步去噪过程，严重限制了其在实时应用中的部署与交互能力，亟需一种单步、高效的个性化图像生成方案。

Method: 提出SwiftPie框架，核心包括：（1）双分支身份注入机制（dual-branch identity injection mechanism），将主体身份信息有效整合到一步扩散模型中；（2）掩码引导的重缩放策略（mask-guided rescaling strategy），在单步扩散内进一步增强主体的上下文融合。

Result: 大量实验表明，SwiftPie在实现卓越个性化生成速度的同时，在身份保真度和提示对齐方面取得了与多步方法相当的性能。

Conclusion: SwiftPie作为首个一步扩散图像个性化工具，为实时、高质量的个性化图像生成提供了新范式，为交互式视觉合成铺平了道路。

Abstract: Diffusion models have achieved remarkable success in high-quality image synthesis, sparking interest in image-guided generation tasks such as subject-driven image personalization. Despite their impressive personalization results, existing methods typically rely on computationally intensive fine-tuning, iterative optimization, or multi-step denoising processes, which significantly hinder their deployment and interactive capability in real-time applications. In this work, we present SwiftPie, the first one-step diffusion image personalization tool that enables lightning-fast generation of personalized images. SwiftPie introduces a novel dual-branch identity injection mechanism that effectively integrates subject identity into a one-step diffusion model. In addition, we incorporate a mask-guided rescaling strategy to further enhance subject contextualization within a single diffusion step. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SwiftPie not only delivers superior image personalization speed but also achieves comparable performance with multi-step approaches in both identity fidelity and prompt alignment. This work opens new opportunities for real-time, high-quality personalized image generation, paving the way for interactive visual synthesis.

</details>


### [57] [Two-Pass Zero-Shot Temporal-Spatial Grounding of Rare Traffic Events in Surveillance Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01512)
*Jiantang Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种无需微调的管道方法，利用冻结的视觉语言模型对真实CCTV交通事故视频进行时间、空间和碰撞类型的联合定位。通过粗到细的两遍分解和专家角色分配，在ACCIDENT@CVPR 2026基准上达到ACC^S=0.539，比基准论文的最佳基线高出0.127，每次视频最多3次API调用，总运行成本约20美元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实CCTV交通事故场景中，标注事故视频通常被禁止，且需要同时对时间、空间和碰撞类型进行精确定位，这是一个罕见事件问题。现有方法难以在无需微调的情况下实现准确的联合输出。

Method: 1) 粗到细的两遍分解：第一遍以1 fps对全视频进行粗粒度(t,x,y,c)元组估计；第二遍在±3秒窗口内以5 fps细化时间和位置，并设置两个确定性置信门控（边界犹豫和边缘钳位坐标）回退到粗估计。2) 专家角色分配：Qwen3-VL-Plus负责定位，Gemini 3.1 Flash-Lite负责对中心化视频片段进行碰撞类型分类。3) 最多3次API调用，17%因API失败回退到物理模型。

Result: 在ACCIDENT@CVPR 2026基准（2,027个真实CCTV视频）上，ACC^S=0.539（95% CI [0.525, 0.553]），比基准论文最佳基线（0.412）高0.127，比最强单VLM基线Molmo-7B（0.396）高0.143，比朴素基线（0.289）高0.250。总运行成本约20美元。

Conclusion: 证明了无需微调、仅通过冻结VLM的粗到细分解和专家角色分配即可显著提升交通事故联合定位性能，且成本极低（约20美元），为真实CCTV场景下的罕见事件定位提供了高效实用的方案。

Abstract: Grounding traffic accidents in real CCTV footage is a rare-event problem where training on labeled accident video is often prohibited, yet accurate joint localization in time, space, and collision type is required. We present a no-fine-tuning pipeline that elicits this joint output from frozen vision-language models through two ideas. First, a coarse-to-fine two-pass decomposition: a full-video pass at 1 fps produces a coarse (t, x, y, c) tuple, then a second pass at 5 fps within a +/- 3 s window refines time and location, with two deterministic confidence gates that revert to the coarse estimate on boundary hedges or edge-clamped coordinates. Second, a specialist role assignment: Qwen3-VL-Plus handles grounding, Gemini 3.1 Flash-Lite handles typing on a centered video clip. On the ACCIDENT@CVPR 2026 benchmark (2,027 real CCTV videos) we reach ACC^S = 0.539 (95% CI [0.525, 0.553]): +0.127 over the benchmark paper's best-of-baselines oracle (0.412), +0.143 over the strongest single-VLM baseline (Molmo-7B, 0.396), and +0.250 over the naive baseline (0.289). The VLM path uses up to three API calls per video (17% fall back to physics on API failures); the full run costs ~$20.

</details>


### [58] [MIRL: Mutual Information-Guided Reinforcement Learning for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01520)
*Yin Zhang,Jiaxuan Zhao,Zonghan Wu,Zengxiang Li,Junfeng Fang,Kun Wang,Qingsong Wen,Yilei Shao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MIRL是一个解耦框架，通过互信息作为预筛选信号，智能分配采样预算并解耦视觉感知与推理阶段的奖励，在减少25%完整轨迹的情况下超越基线，在六个视觉语言推理基准上达到70.22%的平均准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLVR方法在视觉语言推理中面临两大问题：一是大量采样预算浪费在因早期视觉描述错误注定失败的轨迹上；二是稀疏奖励无法区分失败源于视觉感知阶段还是推理阶段。

Method: 提出MIRL解耦框架，利用生成描述与视觉输入之间的互信息作为低成本预筛选信号，通过分叉机制将预算分配给高潜力轨迹；同时进行解耦训练，为视觉感知优化提供独立的基于互信息的奖励，解决奖励盲区问题。

Result: 在六个视觉语言推理基准上，MIRL达到70.22%平均准确率，仅用10个预采样和top-6选择（比16条完整轨迹少25%）即可超越完整采样的性能。

Conclusion: MIRL通过互信息指导的预算分配和解耦奖励机制，有效提升了视觉语言推理中强化学习的效率和准确性。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) frequently suffer from visual perception errors and hallucinations that compromise answer accuracy in complex reasoning tasks. Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) offers a promising solution by optimizing policies using answer correctness signals. Despite their effectiveness, prevailing RLVR methods face two critical limitations. First, much of the sampling budget is wasted on trajectories doomed to fail due to early visual description errors. Second, sparse rewards cannot distinguish whether failures stem from visual perception or reasoning stages. We introduce MIRL, a decoupled framework that addresses both limitations by leveraging mutual information (MI) between generated descriptions and visual inputs as a cheap pre-screening signal. This enables intelligent budget allocation toward high-potential trajectories via forking, while decoupled training provides independent MI-based rewards for visual perception optimization, resolving reward blindness. Experiments on six vision-language reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that MIRL achieves 70.22% average accuracy and successfully surpasses the performance of sampling 16 complete trajectories using only 10 pre-samples with top-6 selection (25% fewer complete trajectories). Our code is available at: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/mirl-main/.

</details>


### [59] [Robust Fundamental Matrix Estimation from Single Image Motion Blur](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01552)
*Bao-Long Tran,Per-Erik Forssén,Fredrik Viksten*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出从单张运动模糊图像中提取基础矩阵的新任务，通过模糊轨迹中的对应关系推断相机在曝光期间的3D运动，并针对时间方向模糊性改进了估计算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法无法从单张运动模糊图像中直接提取描述相机3D运动的基础矩阵，而模糊轨迹中蕴含着相机运动的约束信息。

Method: 1) 建立曝光窗口内两个时间实例间的对应关系；2) 针对时间方向模糊性修改基础矩阵估计算法；3) 引入模糊模式预测的不确定性度量，并将其用于估计器的采样过程。

Result: 在合成和真实运动模糊数据集上验证了方法有效性，能够从单帧图像估计编码3D相机运动的基础矩阵，并在运动分割下游任务中展示了实用性。

Conclusion: 首次证明从单张运动模糊图像估计基础矩阵的可行性，所提方法可有效处理时间方向模糊性问题，为运动分析提供了新思路。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce a challenging task: extracting a fundamental matrix from a single motion blurred image. For a camera moving in 3D during exposure, the smear paths in the blurry image contain cues and constraints on this motion. We demonstrate the feasibility of establishing correspondences between two time instances within the camera exposure window, and that these can be used to robustly infer a fundamental matrix, which summarizes the motion of the camera during the exposure time. The inferred fundamental matrix is unique up to a transpose, corresponding to an ambiguity of the direction of time. Due to this per-smear ambiguity, classic methods, such as the 8-point algorithm, are no longer usable. The proposed method modifies the estimation to work on time-direction ambiguous correspondences. To improve the robustness of the fundamental matrix estimation, we also propose to incorporate an uncertainty measurement in smear pattern prediction and use it in the sampling process of the estimator. Experiments on synthetic and real-world motion-blur datasets demonstrate that our approach is able to estimate the fundamental matrix encoding the 3D camera motion, from single frames. Practical applicability is demonstrated on the downstream task of motion segmentation.

</details>


### [60] [Multi-Dataset Cross-Domain Knowledge Distillation for Unified Medical Image Segmentation, Classification, and Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01563)
*Ceausescu Ciprian-Mihai,Anghelina Ion-Marian,Alexe Dumitru-Bogdan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出统一跨域迁移学习框架，通过教师-学生范式和多级知识蒸馏，在多种医学影像数据集上同时提升分割、分类和检测任务的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用多个异质医学影像数据集的领域不变表征，提升模型在分布偏移下的鲁棒性和泛化能力，实现跨任务、跨模态的统一多任务学习。

Method: 采用教师-学生范式：联合教师模型聚合来自多个源数据集的领域不变表征，任务特定学生模型通过多级知识蒸馏进行训练。框架支持分割、分类和检测任务，实现通用多任务公式。

Result: 在6个分割基准（MRI和CT模态）、多个分类数据集（肺部疾病和痴呆症）及检测数据集上，该方法一致优于强数据集特定基线和多头基线，展现出对分布偏移的更强鲁棒性和更优泛化能力。

Conclusion: 多数据集知识蒸馏是一种可扩展、任务无关的有效方法，能够提升异质医学影像域中分割、分类和检测任务的性能。

Abstract: We propose a unified cross-domain transfer learning framework that leverages knowledge from multiple heterogeneous medical imaging datasets to improve performance across segmentation, classification, and object detection tasks. Our approach employs a teacher-student paradigm in which a joint teacher model aggregates domain-invariant representations learned from diverse source datasets, while a task-specific student model is trained via multi-level knowledge distillation. Originally developed for medical image segmentation, the framework is extended to support image-level classification and object-level detection, enabling a general multi-task formulation for medical image analysis. We evaluate our method on a broad suite of datasets, including six segmentation benchmarks, BrainMetShare, ISLES, BraTS (MRI) and Lung MSD, LiTS, KiTS (CT), as well as multiple classification datasets for pulmonary disease and dementia, and detection datasets with native bounding-box annotations. Across all tasks and modalities, the proposed approach yields consistent improvements over strong dataset-specific and multi-head baselines, demonstrating enhanced robustness to distributional shifts and superior generalization. These findings highlight the potential of multi-dataset knowledge distillation as a scalable and task-agnostic approach for enhancing segmentation, classification, and object detection performance across heterogeneous medical imaging domains.

</details>


### [61] [Unifying Deep Stochastic Processes for Image Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01568)
*Wojciech Kozłowski,Radosław Kuczbański,Kamil Adamczewski,Karol Szczypkowski,Maciej Zięba*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了随机图像增强的统一视角，将现有方法归类为无条件扩散模型、Ornstein-Uhlenbeck过程和扩散桥三种连续时间过程，揭示了它们源自共同的随机微分方程（SDE）公式，并通过受控实验和开源库ItoVision验证了各设计选择的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有随机图像增强方法多样，但不同条件化随机过程与标准扩散模型之间的关系尚不明确，缺乏统一的视角来理解和比较这些方法。

Method: 提出一个统一的SDE框架，将现有方法分为三类连续时间过程（无条件扩散模型、OU过程、扩散桥），指出它们在漂移项、扩散项、终端分布和边界条件上的差异，并通过受控实验（相同架构和训练协议）在多任务上比较性能。

Result: 没有发现某一方法在所有任务上一致占优；识别出对性能影响最大的具体设计选择，并将其与调度器和采样器等正交设计解耦。

Conclusion: 统一框架阐明了不同随机图像增强方法的本质联系，揭示了关键设计因素，并提供了模块化库ItoVision以促进公平比较和快速原型开发。

Abstract: Deep stochastic processes have recently become a central paradigm for image enhancement, with many methods explicitly conditioning the stochastic trajectory on the degraded input. However, the relationship between these conditional processes and standard diffusion models remains unclear. In this work, we introduce a unified perspective on stochastic image enhancement by classifying recent methods into three families of continuous-time processes: unconditional diffusion models, Ornstein-Uhlenbeck (OU) processes, and diffusion bridges. We show that all of these approaches arise from a common stochastic differential equation (SDE) formulation. This framework makes explicit that seemingly disparate methods differ primarily in their drift and diffusion terms, terminal distributions, and boundary conditions, while schedulers and samplers constitute orthogonal design choices. Leveraging this unification, we conduct a controlled empirical study across multiple image enhancement tasks using identical architectures and training protocols. Our results reveal no consistently dominant method; instead, we identify and disentangle the specific design choices that most strongly influence performance. Finally, we release ItoVision, a modular PyTorch library that implements the unified framework and enables rapid prototyping and fair comparison of stochastic image enhancement methods.

</details>


### [62] [Act2See: Emergent Active Visual Perception for Video Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01657)
*Martin Q. Ma,Yuxiao Qu,Aditya Agrawal,Willis Guo,Paul Pu Liang,Ruslan Salakhutdinov,Louis-Philippe Morency*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Act2See是一个使视觉语言模型能够在推理过程中主动插入视频帧的框架，通过监督微调高质量推理轨迹数据集实现，在多个视频推理基准上取得了最先进的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉语言模型通常依赖静态初始帧进行视频推理，无法在推理过程中获取动态信息；现有方法在链式思维中引入帧信息的方式CoT质量欠佳，且缺乏对假设或反事实场景视觉信息的合成能力。

Method: 通过在前沿VLM生成的推理轨迹数据集上进行监督微调（SFT），这些轨迹集成了主动检索现有帧或生成新帧的调用，并经过人工标注CoT验证以确保质量，使模型在推理时能自主决定何时搜索或合成所需视觉证据。

Result: 在VideoEspresso、ViTIB基准上达到最先进水平，在Video-MME、EgoNormia和VCR-Bench上超越同规模或更大模型。

Conclusion: Act2See成功赋予了VLM主动视觉感知能力，实现了视频推理中动态视觉信息的主动获取与合成，是该领域的重要进展。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) typically rely on static initial frames for video reasoning, restricting their ability to incorporate essential dynamic information as the reasoning process evolves. Existing methods that augment Chain-of-Thought (CoT) with additional frame information often exhibit suboptimal CoT quality and lack the crucial ability to synthesize visual information for hypothetical or counterfactual scenarios. We introduce Act-to-See (Act2See), a novel framework that enables active visual perception by empowering VLMs to actively interleave video frames within text CoTs. Act2See is developed via Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) on a high-quality dataset of reasoning traces generated by a frontier VLM. These traces integrate active calls to either retrieve existing frames or generate new ones, and are rigorously verified against human-annotated CoTs to ensure quality. This approach cultivates an emergent capability: at inference time, the model actively determines when to search for or synthesize the necessary visual evidence. Act2See establishes new state-of-the-art results on challenging benchmarks, including VideoEspresso and ViTIB, and outperforms comparable or larger models on Video-MME, EgoNormia, and VCR-Bench, demonstrating an advancement in enabling VLMs with active visual perception for video reasoning.

</details>


### [63] [TRIMMER: A New Paradigm for Video Summarization through Self-Supervised Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01659)
*Pritam Mishra,Coloma Ballester,Dimosthenis Karatzas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TRIMMER，一种自监督强化学习框架用于视频摘要，通过两阶段学习（自监督表征学习+信息论奖励驱动的强化学习）实现无需人工标注的高效视频摘要，在标准基准上达到自监督/无监督方法中的最优性能，且与有监督方法竞争力相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频摘要方法存在三个关键问题：（1）依赖昂贵的人工标注；（2）跨域泛化能力差；（3）复杂架构导致计算成本高。同时，无监督和弱监督方法在捕捉长程时序依赖和语义结构方面通常不如有监督方法。

Method: TRIMMER采用两阶段框架：第一阶段通过自监督学习学习鲁棒的视频表征；第二阶段利用基于信息论奖励函数的强化学习进行时空决策。关键创新包括：（1）引入基于熵的指标替代传统的相似度目标，以捕获高阶时序动态和语义多样性；（2）直接在选定的帧索引上计算奖励，提高计算效率。

Result: 在标准基准上的大量实验表明，TRIMMER在无监督和自监督方法中达到最先进水平，同时与领先的有监督方法相比具有竞争力，验证了其在可扩展和泛化视频摘要方面的有效性。

Conclusion: TRIMMER通过自监督学习和信息论奖励驱动的强化学习组合，有效解决了视频摘要中对标注数据的依赖和计算效率问题，为可扩展、泛化的视频摘要提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: The rapid growth of video content across domains such as surveillance, education, and social media has made efficient content understanding increasingly critical. Video summarization addresses this challenge by generating concise yet semantically meaningful representations, but existing approaches often rely on expensive manual annotations, struggle to generalize across domains, and incur significant computational costs due to complex architectures. Moreover, unsupervised and weakly supervised methods typically underperform compared to supervised counterparts in capturing long-range temporal dependencies and semantic structure. In this work, we propose TRIMMER (Temporal Relative Information Maximization for Multi-objective Efficient Reinforcement), a novel self-supervised reinforcement learning framework for video summarization. TRIMMER operates in two stages: it first learns robust representations via self-supervised learning and then performs spatio-temporal decision making through reinforcement learning guided by information-theoretic reward functions. Unlike prior approaches that rely on similarity-based objectives, our method introduces entropy-based metrics to capture higher-order temporal dynamics and semantic diversity, while computing rewards directly over selected frame indices to improve computational efficiency. Extensive experiments on standard benchmarks demonstrate that TRIMMER achieves state-of-the-art performance among unsupervised and self-supervised methods, while remaining competitive with leading supervised approaches, highlighting its effectiveness for scalable and generalizable video summarization.

</details>


### [64] [Video Active Perception: Effective Inference-Time Long-Form Video Understanding with Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01662)
*Martin Q. Ma,Willis Guo,Aditya Agrawal,Ankit Gupta,Paul Pu Liang,Ruslan Salakhutdinov,Louis-Philippe Morency*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Video Active Perception (VAP)，一种无需训练的方法，通过将关键帧选择视为主动感知中的数据获取过程，并利用轻量级文本条件视频生成模型表示先验世界知识，显著提升长视频问答中帧选择的有效性和效率，在多个基准上实现SOTA零样本结果，每问题帧效率提升达5.6倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大型视觉语言模型在处理长视频问答时，面临帧选择困难：均匀采样成本高且性能易达瓶颈，亟需一种高效且有效的关键帧选取方法。

Method: 受主动感知理论启发，将关键帧选择建模为数据获取过程；利用轻量级文本条件视频生成模型作为先验世界知识，通过计算预期与实际的差异来指导帧的筛选；无需额外训练，直接应用于现有VLM。

Result: 在EgoSchema、NExT-QA、ActivityNet-QA、IntentQA、CLEVRER等长视频/推理视频QA数据集上取得零样本SOTA；相比GPT-4o、Gemini 1.5 Pro、LLaVA-OV，每问题帧效率提升高达5.6倍；推理能力优于先前方法。

Conclusion: 主动感知机制能有效提升长视频问答中帧选择的效率与有效性，为低成本高质量视频理解提供新范式。

Abstract: Large vision-language models (VLMs) have advanced multimodal tasks such as video question answering (QA). However, VLMs face the challenge of selecting frames effectively and efficiently, as standard uniform sampling is expensive and performance may plateau. Inspired by active perception theory, which posits that models gain information by acquiring data that differs from their expectations, we introduce Video Active Perception (VAP), a training-free method to enhance long-form video QA using VLMs. Our approach treats keyframe selection as data acquisition in active perception and leverages a lightweight text-conditioned video generation model to represent prior world knowledge. Empirically, VAP achieves state-of-the-art zero-shot results on long-form or reasoning video QA datasets such as EgoSchema, NExT-QA, ActivityNet-QA, IntentQA, and CLEVRER, achieving an increase of up to 5.6 x frame efficiency by frames per question over standard GPT-4o, Gemini 1.5 Pro, and LLaVA-OV. Moreover, VAP shows stronger reasoning abilities than previous methods and effectively selects keyframes relevant to questions. These findings highlight the potential of leveraging active perception to improve the frame effectiveness and efficiency of long-form video QA.

</details>


### [65] [IMPACT-HOI: Supervisory Control for Onset-Anchored Partial HOI Event Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01666)
*Haoshen Zhang,Di Wen,Kunyu Peng,David Schneider,Zeyun Zhong,Alexander Jaus,Zdravko Marinov,Jiale Wei,Ruiping Liu,Junwei Zheng,Yufan Chen,Yufeng Zhang,Yuanhao Luo,Lei Qi,Rainer Stiefelhagen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出IMPACT-HOI，一种混合主动框架，通过构建人-物交互结构化事件图来标注以自我为中心的程序视频，利用部分指定、起始锚定事件状态的增量解析，结合信任校准控制器和风险受限执行协议，显著减少人工标注动作并保持高事件匹配率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从人类演示中学习机器人操作需要高质量的结构化监督信号，但现有的以自我为中心视频标注方法缺乏对交互事件结构的精确捕捉，且纯人工标注效率低下。

Method: 将视频标注建模为部分指定、起始锚定事件状态的增量解析过程；信任校准控制器根据标注者行为和经验证据质量，在直接查询、人工确认建议和保守补全之间动态选择；风险受限执行协议利用原子回滚确保人工确认的决策不被冲突的自动更新覆盖。

Result: 9名参与者的用户研究表明：手动标注动作减少13.5%，事件匹配率达到46.67%，在研究的协议下确认字段违规事件为零。

Conclusion: IMPACT-HOI通过混合主动策略有效平衡了人工标注效率和标注质量，为机器人操作学习提供高质量的结构化HOI事件图监督信号。

Abstract: We present IMPACT-HOI, a mixed-initiative framework for annotating egocentric procedural video by constructing structured event graphs for Human-Object Interactions (HOI), motivated by the need for high-quality structured supervision for learning robot manipulation from human demonstration. IMPACT-HOI frames this task as the incremental resolution of a partially specified, onset-anchored event state. A trust-calibrated controller selects among direct queries, human-confirmed suggestions, and conservative completions based on empirical annotator behavior and evidence quality. A risk-bounded execution protocol, utilizing atomic rollback, ensures that human-confirmed decisions are preserved against conflicting automated updates. A user study with 9 participants shows a 13.5% reduction in manual annotation actions, a 46.67% event match rate, and zero confirmed-field violations under the studied protocol. The code will be made publicly available at https://github.com/541741106/IMPACT_HOI.

</details>


### [66] [Deep neural networks with Fisher vector encoding for medical image classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01667)
*Lucas O. Lyra,Antonio E. Fabris,Joao B. Florindo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出将Fisher Vectors（一种无序编码方法）引入混合CNN+ViT架构中，并针对大规模数据集中GMM估计的高计算成本问题提出优化方案，在医学图像分类任务上取得了超越基准的成果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合CNN+ViT模型虽已超越纯CNN方法，但尚未充分探索与更精细特征表示（如无序编码方法）的整合；Fisher Vectors作为一种有效的无序编码方法，在大规模数据集上因GMM估计计算成本过高而应用受限。

Method: 将Fisher Vectors无序编码方法引入混合CNN+ViT架构，并提出一种限制GMM估计成本随数据集规模增长的方法，以解决大规模数据集上的计算瓶颈问题。

Result: 在MedMNIST (v2)全部数据集上超越基准结果，在Clean-CC-CCII和ISIC2018上取得与文献竞争的结果。

Conclusion: 所提出的混合CNN+ViT结合Fisher Vectors的方法适用于小规模和大规模数据集，在医学图像分类任务中表现优异，验证了该方法的有效性和可扩展性。

Abstract: Orderless encoding methods have shown to improve Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs) for image classification in the context of limited availability of data. Additionally, hybrid CNN + Vision Transformers (ViT) models have been recently proposed to address CNN locality bias issues. These models outperformed CNN-only approaches. Despite that, the integration of such hybrid models with more elaborated feature representation can be highly beneficial and remains large unexplored in the literature. In this context, we propose the introduction of an orderless encoding method, Fisher Vectors, to hybrid CNN + ViT architectures, aiming at achieving a model suitable for both small and large datasets. Such enconding method relies on estimating a Gaussian Mixture Model (GMM) on image features. In large datasets, computational costs of the GMM estimation is a limiting factor for the application of Fisher Vectors. Thus, we propose a method to limit the growth of GMM estimation costs as we increase the size of the dataset. We explore the feasibility of our method in the context of medical image classification by appling it to MedMNIST (v2), Clean-CC-CCII and ISIC2018. This collection of datasets contains a wide variety of data scales and modalities. We outperform benchmark results in all MedMNIST (v2) datasets and obtain literature-competitive results in Clean-CC-CCII and ISIC2018.

</details>


### [67] [IMPACT-Scribe: Interactive Temporal Action Segmentation with Boundary Scribbles and Query Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01668)
*Qian Yin,Di Wen,Kunyu Peng,David Schneider,Zeyun Zhong,Alexander Jaus,Zdravko Marinov,Jiale Wei,Ruiping Liu,Junwei Zheng,Yufan Chen,Chen Zhang,Lei Qi,Rainer Stiefelhagen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出IMPACT-Scribe，一种基于修正驱动的闭环框架，用于密集时间标注程序性活动视频。该框架将每次人工修正视为改进人机协作的机会，结合不确定性感知边界标注、局部提案建模、代价感知查询规划、结构化传播和修正驱动自适应。实验表明该方法能在降低标注成本的同时提升标注质量和边界准确性，并改善人机交互体验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密集时间标注程序性活动视频对动作理解和具身智能至关重要，但现有反应式标注工具效率低下，每次修正被视为孤立的编辑，无法复用标注者不确定性和模型可靠性信息，导致标注成本高昂。

Method: IMPACT-Scribe是一个修正驱动的密集标注框架，包含五个核心组件：不确定性感知边界标注监督、局部提案建模、代价感知查询规划、结构化传播和修正驱动自适应。通过闭环设计使每次人工修正都能用于改进未来的人机协作过程。

Result: 实验和人工研究表明，该闭环设计能够提高每次标注投入的质量，增强边界准确性，并随时间推移改善人机交互效果。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了将人工修正作为反馈信号引入标注流程的有效性，通过闭环设计实现了标注效率与质量的协同提升，为密集时序标注工具的发展提供了新思路。

Abstract: Dense temporal annotation of procedural activity videos is vital for action understanding and embodied intelligence but remains labor-intensive due to reactive tools. Each correction is treated as an isolated edit, limiting reuse of information on annotator uncertainty and model reliability. We introduce IMPACT-Scribe, a correction-driven framework for dense labeling that uses each correction to improve future human-machine collaboration. IMPACT-Scribe combines uncertainty-aware boundary scribble supervision, local proposal modeling, cost-aware query planning, structured propagation, and correction-driven adaptation. Experiments and a human study show that this closed-loop design improves labeling quality per effort, enhances boundary accuracy, and fosters better human-machine interaction over time. The code will be made publicly available at https://github.com/BanzQians/IMPACT_AS.

</details>


### [68] [TrajRAG: Retrieving Geometric-Semantic Experience for Zero-Shot Object Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01700)
*Yiyao Wang,Sixian Zhang,Keming Zhang,Xinhang Song,Songjie Du,Shuqiang Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TrajRAG框架，通过检索增强生成技术积累和利用历史导航轨迹中的几何-语义经验，提升零样本目标导航性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本目标导航方法仅利用互联网文本知识，缺乏具身3D经验；且导航中的 episodic 观测被丢弃，无法累积终身经验。

Method: 1) 提出拓扑-极性轨迹表示压缩历史观测；2) 分层分块结构组织相似轨迹为统一摘要；3) 导航时用候选前沿生成多轨迹假设检索相似历史经验；4) 持续合并新经验实现终身学习。

Result: 在MP3D、HM3D-v1和HM3D-v2基准上，TrajRAG有效检索相关几何-语义经验，提升了零样本目标导航性能。

Conclusion: 通过检索增强生成将历史轨迹经验融入大模型推理，是一种有效的终身导航经验积累范式，能提升零样本导航能力。

Abstract: Existing zero-shot Object Goal Navigation (ObjectNav) methods often exploit commonsense knowledge from large language or vision-language models to guide navigation. However, such knowledge arises from internet-scale text rather than embodied 3D experience, and episodic observations collected during navigation are typically discarded, preventing the accumulation of lifelong experience. To this end, we propose Trajectory RAG (TrajRAG), a retrieval-augmented generation framework that enhances large-model reasoning by retrieving geometric-semantic experiences. TrajRAG incrementally accumulates episodic observations from past navigation episodes. To structure these observations, we propose a topological-polar (topo-polar) trajectory representation that compactly encodes spatial layouts and semantic contexts, effectively removing redundancies in raw episodic observations. A hierarchical chunking structure further organizes similar topo-polar trajectories into unified summaries, enabling coarse-to-fine retrieval. During navigation, candidate frontiers generate multiple trajectory hypotheses that query TrajRAG for similar past trajectories, guiding large-model reasoning for waypoint selection. New experiences are continually consolidated into TrajRAG, enabling the accumulation of lifelong navigation experience. Experiments on MP3D, HM3D-v1, and HM3D-v2 show that TrajRAG effectively retrieves relevant geometric-semantic experiences and improves zero-shot ObjectNav performance.

</details>


### [69] [Exploring Entropy-based Active Learning for Fair Brain Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01706)
*Ghazal Danaee,Mélanie Gaillochet,Christian Desrosiers,Herve Lombaert,Sylvain Bouix*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向医学图像分割的公平性感知主动学习框架，通过加权熵选择策略调节不确定性，在保持分割性能的同时显著减少了不同人口统计组之间的性能差异（强偏置下减少75%，弱偏置下减少86%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准不确定性主动学习方法仅关注整体性能指标，忽略了不同敏感属性组之间的性能差异或公平性问题；公平主动学习在分类任务中已有探索，但在医学图像分割领域仍是空白。

Method: 提出公平性感知主动学习框架，采用加权熵选择策略：基于已标注集上各组当前性能估计来调节不确定性；为解耦真实认知不确定性与解剖体积变异，使用掩蔽缩放熵限制在感兴趣区域内；在合成T1加权脑MRI数据上，控制左尾状核偏置强度（强/弱），从平衡和极端不平衡初始标注集开始训练3D U-Net。

Result: 相比随机采样和标准不确定性采样，该方法显著降低了组间性能差异；在最终预算下，相比标准熵，差异度量在强偏置下减少75%，弱偏置下减少86%，始终获得最高的公平缩放性能。

Conclusion: 本研究是医学图像分割领域中公平性主动学习的首批工作之一，为资源受限环境下训练更公平的模型提供了高效策略。

Abstract: Active learning (AL) has emerged as a crucial strategy for reducing the prohibitive costs associated with medical image segmentation. However, standard uncertainty-based AL methods typically focus on maximizing performance metrics, ignoring performance disparities or fairness across groups with sensitive attributes. While fair active learning has been explored in classification tasks, its intersection with medical image segmentation remains unaddressed. In this work, we introduced a fairness-aware active learning framework with a Weighted Entropy selection strategy that modulates uncertainty based on current group-specific performance estimates on the labeled set. To decouple true epistemic uncertainty from anatomical volume variances, we further utilized a masked, scaled entropy restricted to the region of interest. The framework was evaluated on synthetic T1-weighted brain MRIs with controlled left caudate bias in both strong and weak bias settings. A 3D U-Net was trained to segment the left caudate under several AL strategies, starting from both demographically balanced and strongly imbalanced initial labeled sets. Experiments demonstrated that our method markedly reduces performance disparities between groups compared to random sampling and standard uncertainty sampling. By prioritizing poorly segmented subgroups during the AL cycles, our method consistently achieved the highest equity-scaled performance and reduced the disparity metric by 75% (strong bias) and 86% (weak bias) relative to standard entropy at the final budget. Overall, this work is among the first studies on fair AL for medical image segmentation, offering an efficient strategy to train more equitable models in resource-constrained environments.

</details>


### [70] [Dual-branch Robust Unlearnable Examples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01718)
*Xianlong Wang,Hangtao Zhang,Wenbo Pan,Ziqi Zhou,Changsong Jiang,Li Zeng,Xiaohua Jia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种双分支不可学习集成扰动优化方法DUNE，在空间和颜色域分别优化扰动，扩展扰动域以增强鲁棒性，并利用集成策略聚合预训练模型，在CIFAR-10和ImageNet上显著优于12种现有UE方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有不可学习示例方案在面对先进防御时鲁棒性有限，原因在于启发式设计或扰动域范围过窄。

Method: 提出DUNE方法，在空间域和颜色域分别优化扰动，建立扰动与偏移标签的映射；进一步提出不可学习增强集成策略，在双分支优化过程中聚合多种预训练模型。

Result: 在CIFAR-10和ImageNet数据集上，DUNE在7种主流防御下平均测试准确率比12种SOTA UE方案低14.95%至50.82%，鲁棒性显著更优。

Conclusion: DUNE通过扩展扰动域和集成策略有效提升了不可学习示例的鲁棒性，在多种防御下均能保持优异的不可学习性能。

Abstract: Unlearnable examples (UEs) aim to compromise model training by injecting imperceptible perturbations to clean samples. However, existing UE schemes exhibit limited robustness against advanced defenses due to their heuristic design or narrowly scoped domain perturbations. To address this, we propose \texttt{DUNE}, a \underline{\textbf{D}}ual-branch \underline{\textbf{UN}}learnable \underline{\textbf{E}}nsemble perturbation optimization approach. Specifically, \texttt{DUNE} separately optimizes perturbations in the spatial and color domains to establish the mapping between perturbations and shift-induced labels. This design extends the perturbation domain to increase noise intensity for improving robustness and drives the models to learn perturbation-oriented features with degraded generalization, thereby achieving unlearnability. To strengthen \texttt{DUNE}'s performance, we further propose an unlearnability-enhancing ensemble strategy that aggregates diverse pre-trained models during the dual-branch optimization. Extensive experiments on benchmark datasets CIFAR-10 and ImageNet verify that \texttt{DUNE}'s robustness outperforms 12 SOTA UE schemes under 7 mainstream defenses, yielding a lower average test accuracy of 14.95\% to 50.82\%.

</details>


### [71] [SignVerse-2M: A Two-Million-Clip Pose-Native Universe of 25+ Sign Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01720)
*Sen Fang,Hongbin Zhong,Yanxin Zhang,Dimitris N. Metaxas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SignVerse-2M是一个大规模多语言手语姿态数据集，包含约200万个片段，涵盖超过25种手语。该数据集通过DWPose统一预处理流水线将原始视频转为2D姿态序列，解决了现有手语资源在开放世界识别、翻译和姿态驱动视频生成方面的不足，并提供了SignDW Transformer基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大规模手语资源仅提供原始视频-文本对齐监督，缺少与现代姿态驱动范式的统一接口：RGB模型在开放世界中鲁棒性差，而姿态引导生成模型需统一关键点表示（如DWPose），但手语领域缺乏此类可直接对接的姿态原生数据集。

Method: 从公开多语言手语视频资源出发，采用DWPose统一预处理流水线将原始视频转换为2D姿态序列，构建约200万片段、覆盖25+种手语的语料库，并设计了SignDW Transformer基线模型。

Result: 构建了SignVerse-2M数据集，提供了数据构建流水线、任务定义和SignDW Transformer基线，验证了该资源在多语言姿态空间建模中的可行性及其与现代姿态驱动流水线的兼容性。

Conclusion: SignVerse-2M通过统一姿态表示保留了真实世界视频的录制条件和说话人多样性，同时减少外观变化，为开放世界手语识别、翻译和姿态驱动生成提供了统一接口，但仍有局限性需进一步讨论。

Abstract: Existing large-scale sign language resources typically provide supervision only at the level of raw video-text alignment and are often produced in laboratory settings. While such resources are important for semantic understanding, they do not directly provide a unified interface for open-world recognition and translation, or for modern pose-driven sign language video generation frameworks: 1. RGB-based pretrained recognition models depend heavily on fixed backgrounds or clothing conditions during recording, and are less robust in open-world settings than style-agnostic pose-processing models. 2. Recent pose-guided image/video generation models mostly use a unified keypoint representation such as DWPose as their control interface. At present, the sign language field still lacks a data resource that can directly interface with this modern pose-native paradigm while also targeting real-world open scenarios. We present SignVerse-2M, a large-scale multilingual pose-native dataset for sign language pose modeling and evaluation. Built from publicly available multilingual sign language video resources, it applies DWPose in a unified preprocessing pipeline to convert raw videos into 2D pose sequences that can be used directly for modeling, resulting in a consolidated corpus of about two million clips covering more than 25 sign languages. Unlike many laboratory datasets, this resource preserves the recording conditions and speaker diversity of real-world videos while reducing appearance variation through a unified pose representation. Toward this goal, we further provide the data construction pipeline, task definitions, and a simple SignDW Transformer baseline, demonstrating the feasibility of this resource for multilingual pose-space modeling and its compatibility with modern pose-driven pipelines, while discussing the evaluation claims it can support as well as its current limitations.

</details>


### [72] [GEASS: Training-Free Caption Steering for Hallucination Mitigation in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01733)
*Zeshang Li,Shuoyang Zhang,Jiashen Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对视觉语言模型的对象幻觉问题，本文发现自生成字幕并非总是有益，因其存在不对称错误（遗漏远多于捏造，但每个捏造影响更大）。提出GEASS模块，通过门控机制、熵加权和证据阈值提升，根据每个查询动态决定模型消费多少字幕信息，仅需两次额外前向传播即可在多个VLM上稳定优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
