Abstract: Despite the success of Large-Vision Language Models (LVLMs), general optimization objectives (e.g., standard MLE) fail to constrain visual trajectories, leading to language bias and hallucination. To mitigate this, current methods introduce geometric priors from visual experts as additional supervision. However, we observe that such supervision is typically suboptimal: it is biased toward geometric precision and offers limited reasoning utility. To bridge this gap, we propose Perceptual Flow Network (PFlowNet), which eschews rigid alignment with the expert priors and achieves interpretable yet more effective visual reasoning. Specifically, PFlowNet decouples perception from reasoning to establish a self-conditioned generation process. Based on this, it integrates multi-dimensional rewards with vicinal geometric shaping via variational reinforcement learning, thereby facilitating reasoning-oriented perceptual behaviors while preserving visual reliability. PFlowNet delivers a provable performance guarantee and competitive empirical results, particularly setting new SOTA records on V* Bench (90.6%) and MME-RealWorld-lite (67.0%).

</details>


### [145] [SIAM: Head and Brain MRI Segmentation from Few High-Quality Templates via Synthetic Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02737)
*Romain Valabregue,Ines Khemir,Eric Badinet,François Rousseau,Guillaume Auzias,Reuben Dorent*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SIAM是一个仅用6个高质量手动标注模板训练的3D全头部分割框架，可同时分割16种解剖结构（包括脑组织和颅外组织），在8个异构数据集上匹配或超越当前最优方法，并展现出跨对比度一致性和对灰质萎缩的敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于合成训练的脑MRI分割方法依赖数百个自动标注模板，引入系统性偏差且难以灵活整合新解剖结构。

Method: 扩展域随机化到强度和形状两个领域：通过合成图像生成确保对比度多样性，利用高分辨率空间变换建模皮质厚度和深部核团形态的解剖差异。仅使用6个高质量手动标注模板进行训练。

Result: 在包含多种对比度（T1、T2、CT）和广泛年龄范围的8个异构数据集（N=301）上，SIAM在脑结构分割上匹配或超越现有最优方法，同时将自动分割扩展至非脑组织，并展现出更优的跨对比度一致性和对灰质萎缩的敏感性。

Conclusion: SIAM仅用少量高质量模板即可实现全头部分割，消除了对数百个自动标注模板的依赖，同时提高了分割的泛化能力和一致性。

Abstract: Synthetic training has recently advanced brain MRI segmentation by enabling contrast-agnostic models trained entirely on generated data. However, most existing approaches rely on hundreds of automatically labeled templates, introducing systematic biases and limiting their flexibility to incorporate new anatomical structures. We present the Segment It All Model (SIAM), a 3D whole-head segmentation framework for 16 anatomical structures, trained using only six high-quality, manually annotated templates. SIAM extends domain randomization to both intensity and shape domains: synthetic image generation ensures contrast variability, while high-resolution spatial transformations model anatomical differences in cortical thickness and deep nuclei morphology. Unlike prior synthetic models, SIAM simultaneously segments brain as well as extra-cerebral tissues, including cerebrospinal fluid, vessels, dura mater, skull, and skin, enabling fully automated, preprocessing-free analysis. Evaluation across eight heterogeneous datasets (N=301), that include multiple contrasts (T1-weighted, T2-weighted, CT) and span a wide range of ages, demonstrates that SIAM matches or outperforms state-of-the-art methods for brain structures, in addition to extending automated segmentation to non-brain structures. The model also exhibits superior consistency across contrasts and repeated acquisitions, together with improved sensitivity to subtle gray matter atrophy. We openly release the model and the label templates at https://github.com/romainVala/SIAM.

</details>


### [146] [Virtual Scanning for NSCLC Histology: Investigating the Discriminatory Power of Synthetic PET](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02746)
*Fatih Aksu,Laura Ciuffetti,Francesco Di Feola,Filippo Ruffini,Giulia Romoli,Fabrizia Gelardi,Arturo Chiti,Valerio Guarrasi,Paolo Soda*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出利用3D Pix2Pix GAN从CT扫描合成伪PET数据，通过多阶段中间融合架构MINT整合两种模态信息，在714例多中心NSCLC数据集上实现了对肺腺癌与鳞状细胞癌组织学亚型分类性能的提升（AUC从0.489提升至0.591，GMean从0.305提升至0.524），证明了合成PET作为CT特征增强策略的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: [¹⁸F]FDG PET/CT虽是非小细胞肺癌（NSCLC）组织学亚型（ADC与SCC）鉴别的重要工具，但存在成本高和辐射暴露的局限。本文旨在探索是否可以通过生成合成PET数据作为特征增强策略，在无需真实PET扫描的情况下为CT提供互补的代谢特征表征，从而提升组织学亚型分类性能。

Method: 提出一个两阶段框架：(1) 使用3D Pix2Pix GAN（在FDG-PET/CT Lesions数据集上预训练）从解剖CT扫描合成伪PET体数据；(2) 将合成PET与结构CT数据通过MINT（多阶段中间融合架构）进行整合，实现跨模态特征融合。

Result: 在714例多中心数据集上，相比仅使用CT的基线，多模态方法将AUC从0.489显著提升至0.591，几何均值（GMean）从0.305提升至0.524，且提升具有统计学显著性。

Conclusion: 合成PET扫描能为深度学习模型提供具有判别能力的代谢线索，支持跨模态互补信息的利用；该特征增强策略在物理PET扫描不可用的临床场景中具有潜在应用价值。

Abstract: Accurate histological differentiation between adenocarcinoma (ADC) and squamous cell carcinoma (SCC) is critical for personalized treatment in non-small cell lung cancer (NSCLC). While [$^{18}$F]FDG PET/CT is a standard tool for the clinical evaluation of lung cancer, its utility is often limited by high costs and radiation exposure. In this paper, we investigate the feasibility of "virtual scanning" as a feature-enhancement strategy by evaluating whether synthetic PET data can provide complementary feature representations to supplement anatomical CT scans in histological subtype classification.
  We propose a framework that leverages a 3D Pix2Pix Generative Adversarial Network (GAN), pretrained on the FDG-PET/CT Lesions dataset, to synthesize pseudo-PET volumes from anatomical CT scans. These synthetic volumes are integrated with structural CT data within the MINT framework, a multi-stage intermediate fusion architecture.
  Our experiments, conducted on a multi-center dataset of 714 subjects, demonstrate that the inclusion of synthetic metabolic features significantly improves classification performance over a CT-only baseline. The multimodal approach achieved a statistically significant increase in the Area Under the Curve (AUC) from 0.489 to 0.591 and improved the Geometric Mean (GMean) from 0.305 to 0.524. These results suggest that synthetic PET scans provide discriminatory metabolic cues that enable deep learning models to exploit complementary cross-modal information, offering a potential feature-enhancement strategy for clinical scenarios where physical PET scans are unavailable.

</details>


### [147] [Does it Really Count? Assessing Semantic Grounding in Text-Guided Class-Agnostic Counting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02752)
*Giacomo Pacini,Luca Ciampi,Nicola Messina,Nicola Tonellotto,Giuseppe Amato,Fabrizio Falchi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文指出现有开放世界文本引导的类无关计数（CAC）方法存在文本语义与视觉对象表征之间的错位问题，并提出了一套新的评估框架PrACo++和数据集MUCCA，系统性地测试模型在负标签测试和干扰物测试下的鲁棒性和可信度。实验发现，尽管当前SOTA方法在标准计数指标上表现良好，但在理解和对齐目标类别描述方面存在显著缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CAC评估协议仅关注单类别图像中的标准计数误差，忽视了模型能否根据文本提示正确对视觉场景中的目标类别进行定位这一基本要求。作者发现多个SOTA模型经常难以判断应计数哪个类别，存在语义与视觉表征错位，导致虚假计数响应和可靠性下降。

Method: (i) 提出PrACo++测试套件，包含负标签测试（negative-label test）和干扰物测试（distractor test）两种专用评估协议及新指标；(ii) 构建MUCCA数据集，每张真实场景图像包含多个标注对象类别，不同于现有单类别基准；(iii) 对10种SOTA方法进行系统实验评估。

Result: 10种SOTA方法在标准计数指标上表现良好，但在理解和对齐目标类别描述任务中表现出显著缺陷。进一步量化分析了提示词之间的语义相似性如何影响这些失败模式。

Conclusion: 当前开放世界文本引导CAC方法亟需更语义化的架构设计，本文提供的评估框架为未来方法在鲁棒性和可信度方面的评估提供了可靠基础。

Abstract: Open-world text-guided class-agnostic counting (CAC) has emerged as a flexible paradigm for counting arbitrary object classes by using natural language prompts. However, current evaluation protocols primarily focus on standard counting errors within single-category images, overlooking a fundamental requirement: the ability to correctly ground the textual prompt in the visual scene. In this paper, we show that several state-of-the-art CAC models often struggle to determine which object class should be counted based on the given prompt, revealing a misalignment between textual semantics and visual object representations. This limitation leads to spurious counting responses and reduced reliability in real-world scenarios. To systematically address these limitations, we propose a new evaluation framework focused on model robustness and trustworthiness. Our contribution is two-fold: (i) we introduce PrACo++ (Prompt-Aware Counting++), a novel test suite featuring two dedicated evaluation protocols -- the negative-label test and the distractor test -- paired with new specialized metrics; and (ii) we present the MUCCA (MUlti-Category Class-Agnostic counting) evaluation dataset, a new collection of real-world images featuring multiple annotated object categories per scene, unlike existing CAC benchmarks that typically include a single category per image. Our extensive experimental evaluation of 10 state-of-the-art methods shows that, despite strong performance under standard counting metrics, current models exhibit significant weaknesses in understanding and grounding object class descriptions. Finally, we provide a quantitative analysis of how semantic similarity between prompts influences these failures. Overall, our results underscore the need for more semantically grounded architectures and offer a reliable framework for future assessment in open-world text-guided CAC methods.

</details>


### [148] [Seeing Realism from Simulation: Efficient Video Transfer for Vision-Language-Action Data Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02757)
*Chenyu Hui,Xiaodi Huang,Siyu Xu,Yunke Wang,Shan You,Fei Wang,Tao Huang,Chang Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种高效视频增强框架，将仿真VLA视频转换为逼真训练视频，通过结构化条件提取、字幕重写、条件视频迁移模型以及扩散特征复用和核心集采样策略，在多个基准和真实机器人平台上显著提升了VLA模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型依赖大规模真实世界视频，而仿真数据虽廉价易得但存在视觉域差和环境多样性不足的问题，导致真实世界泛化能力弱。

Method: 1) 从仿真中提取结构化条件（视频语义分割+视频字幕）；2) 重写字幕以多样化环境；3) 使用条件视频迁移模型合成逼真视频；4) 引入扩散特征复用机制加速生成；5) 采用核心集采样策略选择紧凑非冗余子集进行增强。

Result: 在Robotwin 2.0上提升RDT-1B 8%，在LIBERO-Plus上提升π₀ 5.1%，并在多个基准和真实机器人平台上取得一致性改进。

Conclusion: 该框架能有效弥合仿真与真实之间的视觉域差，以低成本提升VLA模型在真实环境中的泛化能力。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models typically rely on large-scale real-world videos, whereas simulated data, despite being inexpensive and highly parallelizable to collect, often suffers from a substantial visual domain gap and limited environmental diversity, resulting in weak real-world generalization. We present an efficient video augmentation framework that converts simulated VLA videos into realistic training videos while preserving task semantics and action trajectories. Our pipeline extracts structured conditions from simulation via video semantic segmentation and video captioning, rewrites captions to diversify environments, and uses a conditional video transfer model to synthesize realistic videos. To make augmentation practical at scale, we introduce a diffusion feature-reuse mechanism that reuses video tokens across adjacent timesteps to accelerate generation, and a coreset sampling strategy that identifies a compact, non-redundant subset for augmentation under limited computation. Extensive experiments on Robotwin 2.0, LIBERO, LIBERO-Plus, and a real robotic platform demonstrate consistent improvements. For example, our method improves RDT-1B by 8% on Robotwin 2.0, and boosts $π_0$ by 5.1% on the more challenging LIBERO-Plus benchmark. Code is available at: https://github.com/nanfangxiansheng/Seeing-Realism-from-Simulation.

</details>


### [149] [FoR-Net: Learning to Focus on Hard Regions for Efficient Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02764)
*Hsin-Jui Pan,Sheng-Wei Chan,Meng-Qian Li,Chun-Po Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FoR-Net提出了一种轻量级语义分割架构，通过学习重要性图和Top-K激活机制聚焦难例区域（如细长结构和边界），在Cityscapes上以有限计算资源取得了有竞争力的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轻量级语义分割方法依赖全局建模，计算开销大；本文旨在通过区域聚焦推理（region-focused reasoning）替代全局建模，以更高效的策略提升难例区域的分割质量。

Method: 1）Selector模块预测区域级重要性图；2）Top-K激活机制筛选重要区域聚焦处理；3）多分支卷积结构（不同感受野）实现多尺度上下文聚合。

Result: 在Cityscapes上，FoR-Net在有限计算资源下取得与竞品相当的语义分割性能，且在薄结构、边界等挑战性区域表现出更好的一致性。

Conclusion: 区域聚焦推理作为一种简单有效的归纳偏置，能够在轻量级设计中实现高效语义分割，为资源受限场景下的模型设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: We present FoR-Net, a lightweight architecture for semantic segmentation that focuses on identifying and enhancing hard regions. Instead of relying on heavy global modeling, FoR-Net adopts an efficient strategy that selectively emphasizes informative regions through a learned importance map and a Top-K activation mechanism. Specifically, a selector module predicts region-wise importance, enabling the model to focus on challenging areas such as thin structures and object boundaries. Multi-scale reasoning is achieved using convolutional branches with different receptive fields, allowing diverse spatial context aggregation. We evaluate FoR-Net on the Cityscapes benchmark under limited computational resources. Despite its lightweight design and standard training configuration, FoR-Net achieves competitive performance and demonstrates improved consistency in challenging regions. These results suggest that region-focused reasoning provides a simple yet effective inductive bias for efficient semantic segmentation.

</details>


### [150] [Linearizing Vision Transformer with Test-Time Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02772)
*Yining Li,Dongchen Han,Zeyu Liu,Hanyi Wang,Yulin Wang,Gao Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出将预训练Softmax注意力Transformer的权重迁移到线性复杂度注意力架构（TTT）中，通过架构对齐（TTT的双层动态公式与Softmax注意力结构一致）和表示对齐（键实例归一化与局部增强模块）解决权重继承难题。在Stable Diffusion 3.5上验证，仅需1小时微调即可达到可比图像质量，并实现1.32×~1.47×推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性复杂度注意力机制虽能克服Softmax注意力的二次方瓶颈，但其从头训练成本过高。利用预训练Transformer的权重迁移是一个有效捷径，但Softmax注意力与线性注意力之间存在根本性的表示差距，阻碍了权重直接继承。

Method: 从架构对齐和表示对齐两个角度解决转换挑战：1）识别TTT（Test-Time Training）作为线性复杂度架构，其两层动态公式与Softmax注意力在结构上对齐，可直接继承预训练注意力权重；2）引入键实例归一化（key instance normalization）处理键平移不变性，以及轻量级局部增强模块（locality enhancement module）保持局部性，以对齐表示属性。

Result: 在Stable Diffusion 3.5上验证，提出SD3.5-T⁵模型，仅需4×H20 GPU上微调1小时，即可达到与微调后Softmax模型可比的文本到图像质量，同时在1K和2K分辨率下分别实现1.32×和1.47×的推理加速。

Conclusion: 通过架构对齐和表示对齐双管齐下的策略，成功实现了从Softmax注意力到线性复杂度TTT架构的权重迁移，在保持生成质量的同时显著提升了推理效率，为线性注意力模型的实用化提供了一条低成本路径。

Abstract: While linear-complexity attention mechanisms offer a promising alternative to Softmax attention for overcoming the quadratic bottleneck, training such models from scratch remains prohibitively expensive. Inheriting weights from pretrained Transformers provides an appealing shortcut, yet the fundamental representational gap between Softmax and linear attention prevents effective weight transfer. In this work, we address this conversion challenge from two perspectives: architectural alignment and representational alignment. We identify Test-Time Training (TTT) as a linear-complexity architecture whose two-layer dynamic formulation is structurally aligned with Softmax attention, enabling direct inheritance of pretrained attention weights. To further align representational properties, including key shift-invariance and locality, we introduce key instance normalization and a lightweight locality enhancement module. We validate our approach by linearizing Stable Diffusion 3.5 and introduce SD3.5-T$^5$ (Transformer To Test Time Training). With only 1 hour of fine-tuning on 4$\times$H20 GPUs, SD3.5-T$^5$ achieves comparable text-to-image quality to the fine-tuned Softmax model, while accelerating inference by 1.32$\times$ and 1.47$\times$ at 1K and 2K resolutions.

</details>


### [151] [HumanSplatHMR: Closing the Loop Between Human Mesh Recovery and Gaussian Splatting Avatar](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02784)
*Yeheng Zong,Pou-Chun Kung,Yike Pan,Seth Isaacson,Yizhou Chen,Ram Vasudevan,Katherine A. Skinner*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出HumanSplatHMR，一种联合优化框架，通过可微渲染反向传播光度、分割和深度损失来同时优化3D人体姿态和学习高保真虚拟形象，实现了从视频中更准确的人体几何重建和新视角/新姿态合成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法无法准确恢复视频中的人体3D几何：ViT方法不可靠且过拟合2D视图，NeRF和3D高斯泼溅方法将姿态与外观分离处理，限制了新姿态下的泛化能力。

Method: 提出HumanSplatHMR联合优化框架，核心思想是闭环连接几何姿态估计与可微渲染。仅使用SOTA人体姿态估计器输出的人体网格估计，通过可微渲染器反向传播光度、分割和深度损失到姿态参数和全局位置，实现姿态与外观的联合优化。

Result: 在姿态恢复和虚拟形象重建基准上均取得一致性改进，优于省略图像级精度的姿态恢复方法和解耦姿态估计与虚拟形象重建的方法。

Conclusion: 通过将人体姿态既作为形变先验又作为优化目标，闭环连接姿态估计与可微渲染，能够同时提升3D姿态精度和新视角渲染质量，适用于真实场景。

Abstract: Accurately recovering human pose and appearance from video is an essential component of scene reconstruction, with applications to motion capture, motion prediction, virtual reality, and digital twinning. Despite significant interest in building realistic human avatars from video, this paper demonstrates that existing methods do not accurately recover the 3D geometry of humans. ViT-based approaches are not consistently reliable and can overfit to 2D views, while NeRF- and Gaussian Splatting-based avatars treat pose and appearance separately, limiting rendering generalization to new poses. To resolve these shortcomings, this paper proposes HumanSplatHMR, a joint optimization framework that refines 3D human poses while simultaneously learning a high-fidelity avatar for novel-view and novel-pose synthesis. Our key insight is to close the loop between geometric pose estimation and differentiable rendering. Unlike prior human avatar methods that rely on accurate human pose obtained through motion capture systems or offline refinement, which are impractical in in-the-wild scenarios, our approach uses only human mesh estimates from a state-of-the-art human pose estimator to better reflect real-world conditions. Therefore, instead of using the human pose only as a deformation prior, HumanSplatHMR backpropagates photometric, segmentation, and depth losses through a differentiable renderer to the pose parameters and global position. This coupling refines the global 3D pose over time, improving accuracy and alignment while producing better renderings from novel views. Experiments show consistent improvements over pose recovery baselines that omit image-level refinement and avatar baselines that decouple pose estimation from avatar reconstruction.

</details>


### [152] [IConFace: Identity-Structure Asymmetric Conditioning for Unified Reference-Aware Face Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02814)
*Axi Niu,Jinyang Zhang,Senyan Qing*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: IConFace是一个统一的支持参考图像和无参考图像的盲人脸修复框架，通过身份-结构非对称条件机制，在有参考时利用参考改善身份一致性和细节恢复，无参考时退化为纯退化图像修复，使用单一模型同时处理两种场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 盲人脸修复在严重退化下高度病态，身份关键细节可能缺失。相同身份的参考图像可减少歧义，但参考图像与输入图像在姿态、表情、光照、年龄、妆容或局部面部状态上的不匹配会导致参考外观过度使用的问题。

Method: 提出IConFace框架，采用身份-结构非对称条件机制：参考图像通过归一化加权的全局AdaFace身份锚点进行仅图像级调制；退化图像通过低秩残差和带双路径记忆的块级退化交叉注意力增强为空间结构锚点。

Result: 单一检查点在有参考时利用参考改善身份一致性和细节恢复，无参考时退化为无参考修复，同时提升了两类场景下的修复质量。

Conclusion: IConFace通过非对称条件设计统一了参考感知和无参考两种修复范式，有效解决了参考不匹配导致的过度使用问题，并在统一模型中同时提升了身份一致性和细节恢复能力。

Abstract: Blind face restoration is highly ill-posed under severe degradation, where identity-critical details may be missing from the degraded input. Same-identity references reduce this ambiguity, but mismatched pose, expression, illumination, age, makeup, or local facial states can lead to overuse of reference appearance. We propose \textbf{IConFace}, a unified reference-aware and no-reference framework with identity--structure asymmetric conditioning. References are distilled into a norm-weighted global AdaFace identity anchor for image-only modulation, while the degraded image is reinforced as the spatial structure anchor through low-rank residuals and block-wise degraded cross-attention with two-route memory. The resulting single checkpoint exploits references when available and falls back to no-reference restoration when absent, improving identity consistency, fine-detail recovery, and degraded-only restoration quality in a unified model.

</details>


### [153] [VideoNet: A Large-Scale Dataset for Domain-Specific Action Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02834)
*Tanush Yadav,Mohammadreza Salehi,Jae Sung Park,Vivek Ramanujan,Hannaneh Hajishirzi,Yejin Choi,Ali Farhadi,Rohun Tripathi,Ranjay Krishna*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VideoNet基准，用于评估VLM在特定领域动作识别上的能力，发现现有模型表现不佳（Qwen仅45%），即使提供少量示例模型提升仍不如人类（+13.6% vs +7.0%）。作者收集了近50万视频问答对进行微调，使4B模型超越所有8B开源模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前VLM在动作识别能力上缺乏评估，主要因为缺乏多样化、有挑战性的数据。本文旨在重新推动VLM时代对特定领域动作识别的关注与评估。

Method: 构建了包含37个领域、1000种动作的VideoNet基准，采用多项选择、二元选择和少量示例三种评估设置。收集了近50万视频问答对作为训练数据，对Molmo2-4B模型进行微调。

Result: Gemini 3.1 Pro准确率69.9%，Qwen3-VL-8B仅45.0%；二元设置下Qwen为59.2%；提供3个示例时Qwen提升+7.0%，Gemini下降-4.8%，而人类提升+13.6%。微调后的Molmo2-4B超越所有8B开源模型。

Conclusion: VLM在特定领域动作识别上能力不足，且无法像人类一样有效利用上下文示例。通过大规模领域特定数据微调可显著提升模型性能，缩小与小模型的差距。

Abstract: Videos are unique in their ability to capture actions which transcend multiple frames. Accordingly, for many years action recognition was the quintessential task for video understanding. Unfortunately, due to a lack of sufficiently diverse and challenging data, modern vision-language models (VLMs) are no longer evaluated on their action recognition capabilities. To revitalize action recognition in the era of VLMs, we advocate for a returned focus on domain-specific actions. To this end, we introduce VideoNet, a domain-specific action recognition benchmark covering 1,000 distinct actions from 37 domains. We begin with a multiple-choice evaluation setting, where the difference between closed and open models is stark: Gemini 3.1 Pro attains 69.9% accuracy while Qwen3-VL-8B gets a mere 45.0%. To understand why VLMs struggle on VideoNet, we relax the questions into a binary setting, where random chance is 50%. Still, Qwen achieves only 59.2% accuracy. Further relaxing the evaluation setup, we provide $k\in\{1,2,3\}$ in-context examples of the action. Some models excel in the few-shot setting, while others falter; Qwen improves $+7.0\%$, while Gemini declines $-4.8\%$. Notably, these gains fall short of the $+13.6\%$ improvement in non-expert humans when given few-shot examples. Finding that VLMs struggle to fully exploit in-context examples, we shift from test-time improvements to the training side. We collect the first large-scale training dataset for domain-specific actions, totaling nearly 500k video question-answer pairs. Fine-tuning a Molmo2-4B model on our data, we surpass all open-weight 8B models on the VideoNet benchmark.

</details>


### [154] [Active Sampling for Ultra-Low-Bit-Rate Video Compression via Conditional Controlled Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02849)
*Amirhosein Javadi,Shirin Saeedi Bidokhti,Tara Javidi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ActDiff-VC是一种面向超低比特率的扩散视频压缩框架，通过自适应关键帧选择和预算感知的稀疏轨迹选择生成紧凑条件信号，利用条件扩散解码器合成其余帧，在UVG和MCL-JCV基准上相比强学习编解码器实现高达64.6%的比特率节省，并在感知质量指标上显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在超低比特率下为感知重建提供了强大的生成先验，但有效的视频压缩需要使用高度紧凑的条件信号控制生成过程，现有方法在此方面存在不足。

Method: 将视频分割为可变长度片段，仅在需要时传输关键帧，并用一组紧凑的跟踪点轨迹总结时序动态；提出内容自适应关键帧选择和预算感知稀疏轨迹选择机制，生成紧凑有效条件信号，条件扩散解码器据此合成其余帧。

Result: 在UVG和MCL-JCV基准上，与强学习编解码器相比，在匹配NIQE时实现高达64.6%的比特率降低，在可比比特率下KID提升最高64.6%，FID提升最高37.7%，在超低比特率区间呈现优于学习和扩散基线的感知率失真权衡。

Conclusion: ActDiff-VC通过紧凑的轨迹条件信号和自适应机制，在超低比特率下实现了有效的感知视频压缩，证明了扩散模型结合稀疏条件信号在极端压缩场景中的潜力。

Abstract: Diffusion models provide a powerful generative prior for perceptual reconstruction at ultra-low bitrates, but effective video compression requires controlling the generative process using highly compact conditioning signals. In this work, we present ActDiff-VC, a diffusion-based video compression framework for the ultra-low-bitrate regime. Our method partitions videos into variable-length segments, transmits keyframes only when needed, and summarizes temporal dynamics using a compact set of tracked point trajectories. Conditioned on these sparse signals, a conditional diffusion decoder synthesizes the remaining frames, enabling perceptually realistic reconstruction under severe rate constraints. To support this design, we introduce two mechanisms: content-adaptive keyframe selection and budget-aware sparse trajectory selection, which together enable compact yet effective conditioning for generative reconstruction. Experiments on the UVG and MCL-JCV benchmarks show that ActDiff-VC achieves up to 64.6\% bitrate reduction at matched NIQE, improves KID by up to 64.6\% and FID by up to 37.7\% at comparable bitrates against strong learned codecs, and delivers favorable perceptual rate--distortion trade-offs relative to learned and diffusion-based baselines in the ultra-low-bitrate regime.

</details>


### [155] [AlbumFill: Album-Guided Reasoning and Retrieval for Personalized Image Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02892)
*Yu-Ju Tsai,Brian Price,Qing Liu,Luis Figueroa,Daniil Pakhomov,Zhihong Ding,Scott Cohen,Ming-Hsuan Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AlbumFill是一种无需训练的框架，通过从个人相册中检索身份一致的参考图像，利用视觉-语言模型推断缺失语义线索，实现个性化图像补全，并发布了包含5.4万人类中心样本的数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法要么依赖通用修复模型导致身份一致性差，要么假设有明确提供的参考图像，但在实际场景中用户往往不会主动提供合适的参考图，需要系统自动从个人相册中搜索。

Method: 提出AlbumFill框架：1) 使用视觉-语言模型推断遮挡区域的缺失语义线索；2) 基于这些线索引导组合图像检索，从个人相册中召回身份一致的参考图；3) 将检索到的参考图输入基于参考的补全模型进行修复。整个过程无需训练。

Result: 构建了一个包含5.4万张以人为中心的样本及对应相册图像的数据集。实验表明该任务具有挑战性，并验证了身份一致的参考检索对个性化补全的重要性。

Conclusion: AlbumFill有效解决了无显式参考情况下的个性化图像补全问题，证明了无需训练的检索-补全范式在保持身份一致性方面的有效性。

Abstract: Personalized image completion aims to restore occluded regions in personal photos while preserving identity and appearance. Existing methods either rely on generic inpainting models that often fail to maintain identity consistency, or assume that suitable reference images are explicitly provided. In practice, suitable references are often not explicitly provided, requiring the system to search for identity-consistent images within personal photo collections. We present AlbumFill, a training-free framework that retrieves identity-consistent references from personal albums for personalized completion. Given an occluded image and a personal album, a vision-language model infers missing semantic cues to guide composed image retrieval, and the retrieved references are used by reference-based completion models. To facilitate this task, we introduce a dataset containing 54K human-centric samples with associated album images. Experiments across multiple baselines demonstrate the difficulty of personalized completion and highlight the importance of identity-consistent reference retrieval. Project Page: https://liagm.github.io/AlbumFill/

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [156] [H-Probes: Extracting Hierarchical Structures From Latent Representations of Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00847)
*Cutter Dawes,Aryan Sharma,Angelos Ioannis Lagos,Shivam Raval*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出H-probes线性探针，用于提取大语言模型潜在表征中的层次结构（深度和距离）。实验表明，在合成树遍历任务中，层次相关子空间低维、因果重要且泛化性强；在数学推理等真实场景中也发现类似但较弱的层次结构，证明模型不仅在语法层面，更在抽象推理层面表征层次。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在需要层次推理的任务上表现优异，但对其如何在几何空间中表征层次结构的潜在构造缺乏深入分析。

Method: 开发了H-probes线性探针集合，从潜在表征中提取层次结构（深度和成对距离）；在合成树遍历任务和真实数学推理场景中进行实验，并进行全面的消融分析。

Result: H-probes能稳健地提取包含层次结构的子空间；这些子空间低维、对任务性能因果重要、具有域内和域外泛化能力；在真实世界层次化场景（如数学推理）中也发现类似但较弱的层次结构。

Conclusion: 模型不仅在语法和概念层面表征层次，还在更深层次的抽象（包括推理过程本身）中表征层次结构。

Abstract: Representing and navigating hierarchy is a fundamental primitive of reasoning. Large language models have demonstrated proficiency in a wide variety of tasks requiring hierarchical reasoning, but there exists limited analysis on how the models geometrically represent the necessary latent constructions for such thinking. To this end, we develop \textit{H-probes}, a collection of linear probes that extract hierarchical structure, specifically depth and pairwise distance, from latent representations. In synthetic tree traversal tasks, the H-probes robustly find the subspaces containing hierarchical structure necessary to complete the tasks; furthermore, in comprehensive ablation experiments, we show that these hierarchy-containing subspaces are low-dimensional, causally important for high task performance, and generalize within- and out-of-domain. Furthermore, we find analogous, though weaker, hierarchical structure in real-world hierarchical contexts such as mathematical reasoning traces. These results demonstrate that models represent hierarchy not only at the level of syntax and concepts, but at deeper levels of abstraction -- including the reasoning process itself.

</details>


### [157] [Model Organisms Are Leaky: Perplexity Differencing Often Reveals Finetuning Objectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00994)
*Mohammed Abu Baker,Luca Baroni,Dan Wilhelm*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于困惑度（perplexity）的简单方法，通过分析微调模型在随机短前缀下的生成文本，利用其过度泛化微调行为的倾向，无需模型内部信息即可识别微调目标。在76个模型生物体（0.5B-70B参数）上验证，包括后门模型、合成文档微调模型、对抗训练模型等，该方法能有效从排名靠前的生成结果中揭示微调目标，且可使用不同系列的参考模型替代原预训练检查点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微调可能引入有害行为，虽然研究者通过构建模型生物体来研究这些风险，但识别这些微调行为仍然具有挑战性。现有方法往往需要模型内部信息或对行为的先验假设，缺乏一种简单、通用且无需内部信息的检测手段。

Method: 首先，从通用语料库中抽取随机短前缀输入微调模型，生成多样化的补全文本。然后，计算参考模型与微调模型在每组补全上的困惑度差距，并按差距降序排列。排名靠前的补全往往反映了微调目标。该方法仅需微调模型的下一个token概率，兼容仅暴露token logprobs的API模型。

Result: 在76个不同规模的模型生物体（0.5B-70B参数）上测试，包括后门模型、合成文档微调模型、对抗训练模型和涌现失调模型。绝大多数情况下，排名靠前的补全能揭示微调目标。使用不同系列的参考模型替代原始预训练检查点也能有效工作。

Conclusion: 该方法简单有效，无需模型内部信息或先验假设，能够可靠地识别模型生物体中的微调目标，为LLM安全审计提供了一种实用的检测工具。

Abstract: Finetuning can significantly modify the behavior of large language models, including introducing harmful or unsafe behaviors. To study these risks, researchers develop model organisms: models finetuned to exhibit specific known behaviors for controlled experimentation. Identifying these behaviors remains challenging. We show that a simple perplexity-based method can surface finetuning objectives from model organisms by leveraging their tendency to overgeneralize their finetuned behaviors beyond the intended context. First, we generate diverse completions from the finetuned model using short random prefills drawn from general corpora. Second, we rank completions by decreasing perplexity gap between reference and finetuned models. The top-ranked completions often reveal the finetuning objectives, without requiring model internals or prior assumptions about the behavior. We evaluate this on a diverse set of model organisms (N=76, 0.5 to 70B parameters), including backdoored models, models finetuned to internalize false facts via synthetic document finetuning, adversarially trained models with hidden concerning behaviors, and models exhibiting emergent misalignment. For the vast majority of model organisms tested, the method surfaces completions revealing finetuning objectives within the top-ranked results, with models trained via synthetic document finetuning or to produce exact phrases being particularly susceptible. We further show that the technique can be effective even without access to the exact pre-finetuning checkpoint: trusted reference models from different families can serve as effective substitutes. As the method requires only next-token probabilities from the finetuned model, it is compatible with API-gated models that expose token logprobs.

</details>


### [158] [Can AI Debias the News? LLM Interventions Improve Cross-Partisan Receptivity but LLMs Overestimate Their Own Effectiveness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01006)
*Faisal Feroz,Jonas R. Kunst*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现，LLM生成的非政治化处理能够通过改变意识形态框架（而非表层语言）提高跨党派信任，但当前模型在评估自身干预效果时缺乏定量准确性和心理保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 党派新闻媒体侵蚀了跨党派信任，LLM提供了一种大规模去偏见的潜在手段，研究者希望测试LLM生成的非政治化处理能否改善保守派对自由派新闻标题的信任相关判断。

Method: 两个预先注册的实验：研究1测试了微妙的词汇去偏（用更中性的同义词替换情感词）；研究2测试了更实质性的重构干预。同时比较了人类读者和LLM模拟的硅基参与者的反应。

Result: 研究1中词汇去偏无效；研究2中实质性重构显著提高了保守派对自由派新闻标题的可信度、完整性和参与意愿，且未在自由派样本中产生反冲效应。硅基参与者在研究1中显示稳健效果（人类无效果），在研究2中方向一致但效应量更大。模型对谁会对去偏做出反应的隐含理论与实际预测人类反应的心理特征存在分歧。

Conclusion: 基于LLM的去偏处理在针对意识形态框架而非表层语言时能改善跨党派接受度，但当前模型缺乏评估自身干预所需的定量准确性和定性心理保真度，需要人类监督。

Abstract: Partisan news media erode cross-partisan trust, but large language models (LLMs) offer a potential means of debiasing such content at scale. Across two pre-registered experiments, we tested whether LLM-generated debiasing of liberal news headlines could improve conservative readers' trust-relevant judgments. Study 1 found that subtle lexical debiasing (replacing emotive words with more moderate synonyms) had no effect on any outcome. Study 2 found that a more substantive reframing intervention significantly increased conservatives' perceived trustworthiness, completeness, and willingness to engage with liberal news headlines, without producing a backfire effect among a sample of liberals. In Study 1, the intervention produced robust effects among LLM-simulated silicon participants, whereas it had no impact on human readers. In Study 2, the intervention's effects among silicon participants aligned directionally with human responses but were significantly larger in magnitude for some outcomes. Moderation analyses revealed that the model's implicit theory of who responds to debiasing diverged from the psychological profile that actually predicted human responsiveness. These findings demonstrate that LLM-based debiasing can improve cross-partisan receptivity when targeting ideological framing rather than surface-level language, but that current models lack both the quantitative accuracy and qualitative psychological fidelity to evaluate their own interventions without human oversight.

</details>


### [159] [CLEAR: Revealing How Noise and Ambiguity Degrade Reliability in LLMs for Medicine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01011)
*Kevin H. Guo,Chao Yan,Avinash Baidya,Katherine Brown,Xiang Goa,Juming Xiong,Zhijun Yin,Bradley A. Malin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出CLEAR评估框架，系统研究决策空间呈现、模糊性和不确定性对大语言模型在医学基准测试中推理能力的影响。通过对3个基准测试中17个LLM的分析，发现增加合理答案数量会降低模型正确识别和弃权能力；弃权措辞从"以上都不对"转向"我不知道"会加剧不谨慎行为；模型规模扩大反而会加剧"谦逊赤字"（正确识别与放弃错误答案之间的性能差距）。研究表明标准医学基准测试存在局限，仅靠模型扩展无法解决LLM的可靠性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的医学大语言模型评估多采用简化的考试式基准，无法反映真实医疗场景中的模糊性和不确定性，因此需要更贴近临床实际、能系统评估模型在模糊条件下推理能力的评估框架。

Method: 提出CLEAR评估框架，通过三个维度的系统扰动来评估LLM：(1) 改变合理答案选项的数量；(2) 改变真实答案或弃权选项的存在与否；(3) 改变答案选项的语义措辞。在3个医学基准测试上对17个LLM进行系统评估。

Result: (1) 增加合理答案数量会降低模型正确识别答案和放弃错误答案的能力；(2) 弃权措辞从"以上都不对"变为"我不知道"时，模型的不谨慎行为加剧，仅加入IDK选项就增加了错误答案的选择；(3) 提出了"谦逊赤字"概念，即正确识别答案与放弃错误答案之间的性能差距，且该赤字随模型规模扩大而恶化。

Conclusion: 标准医学基准测试存在显著局限性，仅靠模型规模扩展无法解决LLM在医学领域的可靠性问题，需要更精细的评估方法和设计策略来提升模型在模糊医疗场景下的表现。

Abstract: Medical large language model (LLM) evaluations rely on simplified, exam-style benchmarks that rarely reflect the ambiguity of real-world medical inquiries. We introduce the CLinical Evaluation of Ambiguity and Reliability (CLEAR) framework, which assesses how decision-space presentation, ambiguity, and uncertainty affect LLMs' reasoning on medical benchmarks. CLEAR systematically perturbs (1) the number of plausible answer options, (2) the presence of a ground truth or abstention option, and (3) the semantic framing of answer options. Applying CLEAR on three benchmarks evaluated across 17 LLMs reveals three notable limitations of existing evaluation methods. First, increasing the number of plausible answers degrades a model's ability to identify the correct answer and abstain against incorrect ones. Second, this lack of caution intensifies as the framing of abstention shifts from assertive rejection like "None of the Above" to uncertainty admission like "I don't know" (IDK). Notably, just including IDK in the answer space increases incorrect answer selections. Lastly, we formalize the performance gap between identifying the correct answer and abstaining from incorrect ones as the humility deficit, which worsens with model scale. Our findings reveal limitations in standard medical benchmarks and underscore that scaling alone does not resolve LLM reliability issues.

</details>


### [160] [Psychologically Potent, Computationally Invisible: LLMs Generate Social-Comparison Triggers They Fail to Detect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01017)
*Hua Zhao,Jiapei Gu,Michelle Mingyue Gu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出了XHS-SCoRE基准，用于从读者视角检测小红书帖子是否引发向上、向下或中性社会比较。研究发现提示型LLM分类器在检测社会比较信号时存在稳定且可解释的失败模式，尽管生成流畅但检测能力不可靠，且生成内容能影响读者感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社会比较是社交平台上一种行为真实但无法简化为情感分析的社会意义关系信号，现有方法缺乏从读者视角出发的基准来检测这种信号。

Method: 构建了XHS-SCoRE基准（文本型小红书帖子），标注三类社会比较（向上/向下/中性）。对比提示型LLM分类器与监督式中文编码基线模型的表现，并进行受控实验验证LLM生成内容对读者感知的影响。

Result: 提示型LLM分类器表现出稳定的可解释失败模式：倾向于将触发比较的帖子中性化，且存在模型特定的方向性偏差。监督模型在域内表现可学习，但提示分类的鲁棒性不足。LLM生成的内容能改变读者感知和比较相关情感。

Conclusion: 社会比较信号在文本上是可学习的（域内），但提示型分类无法稳健获取。XHS-SCoRE为读者视角的比较检测提供了基准，并为研究社会意义关系线索在提示推理中部分可见的问题提供了诊断框架。

Abstract: We introduce Xiaohongshu Social Comparison Reader Elicitation (XHS-SCoRE), a reader-grounded benchmark for detecting if a text-only Xiaohongshu (RedNote) post elicits UPWARD, DOWNWARD, or NEUTRAL/no clear social comparison from a first-person reader perspective. The task targets a socially meaningful relational signal that is behaviorally real yet not reducible to sentiment. Across prompted LLM classifiers and supervised Chinese encoder baselines, we find a consistent mismatch between generation fluency and reliable detection ability: the signal is textually learnable in-domain, but not robustly accessible to prompt-based classification. Prompted LLM classifiers exhibit stable, interpretable failure modes, especially neutralization of comparison-triggering posts and model-specific directional skew. A controlled pilot further shows that LLM-generated Xiaohongshu-style posts can shift perceived standing and comparison-related affect even when prompt-based detection of the same construct remains fragile. XHS-SCoRE contributes both a benchmark for reader-grounded comparison detection and a diagnostic framework for studying when socially meaningful relational cues remain only partially visible to prompt-based inference.

</details>


### [161] [A Theoretical Game of Attacks via Compositional Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01034)
*Xinbo Wu,Huan Zhang,Abhishek Umrawal,Lav R. Varshney*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个形式化攻击者与防御者博弈的理论框架，设计了理论最优的攻击策略，证明其与现有对抗提示方法密切相关，分析了博弈均衡并揭示了攻击者的固有优势，进而推导出可证明最优的防御策略。实验表明，理论最优攻击的实践实例在多种LLM和基准上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型能力不断增强，但其安全部署面临对抗性提示的威胁，现有对齐策略仍可被绕过，缺乏对攻击-防御博弈的理论化理解和最优策略的形式化分析。

Method: 形式化攻击者与防御者之间的博弈框架；设计理论最优响应攻击策略；分析博弈均衡及攻击者优势；从理论分析推导可证明最优防御策略；在实际LLM和基准上进行实证评估。

Result: 理论最优攻击的实践实例在多种LLM和不同基准上展现出比现有对抗提示方法更强的性能。

Conclusion: 该理论框架不仅形式化了对抗性攻击与防御的博弈关系，还揭示了攻击者的固有优势，并提供了可证明最优的防御策略，为LLM安全对齐提供了理论指导。

Abstract: As large language models grow increasingly capable, concerns about their safe deployment have intensified. While numerous alignment strategies aim to restrict harmful behavior, these defenses can still be circumvented through carefully designed adversarial prompts. In this work, we introduce a theoretical framework that formalizes a game between an attacker and a defender. Within this framework, we design a theoretical best-response attack strategy and show that it is closely related to many existing adversarial prompting methods. We further analyze the resulting game, characterize its equilibria, and reveal inherent advantages for the attacker. Drawing on our theoretical analysis, we also derive a provably optimal defense strategy. Empirically, we evaluate a practical instantiation of the theoretically optimal attack and observe stronger performance relative to existing adversarial prompting approaches in diverse settings encompassing different LLMs and benchmarks.

</details>


### [162] [Compared to What? Baselines and Metrics for Counterfactual Prompting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01048)
*Zihao Yang,Mosh Levy,Yoav Goldberg,Byron C. Wallace*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 反事实提示（如改变患者性别）导致的LLM预测翻转率（14.9%）与简单释义（14.1%）无显著差异，表明观察到的效应不能归因于目标变量。本文提出通过统计检验比较目标干预与释义基线差异的框架，并发现先前MedPerturb数据集中120项测试仅5项显著。该框架能检测到真实的定向性别偏差，且每样本度量比聚合度量更强大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反事实提示被广泛用于评估LLM偏见和思维链忠实性，但每个反事实编辑都是复合处理——捆绑了目标变量与偶然的表层形式变异，违反了处理变量无关性假设。现有方法无法区分目标变量效应与一般模型敏感性，导致归因错误。

Method: 提出一个统计检验框架：将目标干预（如改变性别）下观察到的差异与简单释义（保持意义不变的改写）所引发的差异进行比较。只有当目标干预的效果显著大于释义基线时，才能归因于目标变量。评估了聚合度量、每样本分布度量、回归度量三种指标。

Result: MedQA上改变患者性别导致14.9%翻转率，但与释义的14.1%无统计差异。在MedPerturb数据集中，120项测试仅5项达到统计显著。在职业传记分类任务中，该框架成功检测到显著的定向性别偏差。每样本度量比聚合度量更具统计效力，回归度量能独特地表征效应方向和大小。

Conclusion: 反事实提示必须控制释义基线才能做出有效归因；先前LLM对人口统计特征的敏感性发现可能是虚假的；建议使用每样本度量和回归度量替代聚合度量进行效应分析。

Abstract: Counterfactual prompting (i.e., perturbing a single factor and measuring output change) is widely used to evaluate things like LLM bias and CoT faithfulness. But in this work we argue that observed effects cannot be attributed to the targeted factor without accounting for baseline ``meaning-preserving'' modifications to text that establish general model sensitivity. This is because every counterfactual edit is a compound treatment that bundles the variable of interest with incidental surface-form variation; this violates treatment variation irrelevance. We observe prediction flip rates on MedQA of 14.9% when we surgically change patient gender. However, this is statistically indistinguishable from the flip rates induced by simply paraphrasing inputs (14.1%). In this case, it would therefore be unwarranted to conclude that the LLM is especially sensitive to patient gender. To account for this and robustly measure the effects of targeted interventions, we propose a framework in which we compare (via statistical testing) differences observed under target interventions to those induced by paraphrasing inputs. We then use this framework to revisit a analysis done on the MedPerturb dataset, which reported evidence of model sensitivity to patient demographics and stylistic cues. We find that these effects largely dissipate when we account for general model sensitivity, with only 5 of 120 tests reaching statistical significance. Applying the same framework to occupational biography classification, we detect clearly significant directional gender bias, showing that the framework identifies real directional effects even when they are small. We evaluate a range of metrics -- aggregate, per-sample distributional, and regression -- and find that per-sample metrics are dramatically more powerful than aggregate metrics and regression powerfully and uniquely characterizes effect direction and magnitude.

</details>


### [163] [A Systematic Exploration of Text Decomposition and Budget Distribution in Differentially Private Text Obfuscation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01065)
*Stephen Meisenbacher,Angelo Kleinert,Florian Matthes*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了差分隐私文本混淆中不同的文本分解与隐私预算分配技术，发现即使隐私预算相当，不同的方法组合会导致显著不同的结果，并证明了通过优化DP混淆过程可以最大化经验性权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管词级扰动直观，但有意义的文本私有化发生在完整文档层面。现有研究已为隐私预算分配奠定了基础，但不同文本分块与预算分配方法的系统比较尚缺。

Method: 对多种文本分解（chunking）方法和ε预算分配技术进行系统评估，测试不同分块方式与预算分配策略的组合效果，并通过实验对比不同设计选择下的隐私-效用权衡。

Result: 即使隐私预算相当，不同的文本分解与预算分配方法组合会导致显著不同的结果，表明设计选择至关重要。

Conclusion: 提供了通过优化DP混淆过程来最大化经验性权衡的可行证据。

Abstract: The goal of differentially private text obfuscation is to obfuscate, or "perturb", input texts with Differential Privacy (DP) guarantees, such that the private output texts are quantifiably indistinguishable from the originals. While perturbation at the word level is intuitive, meaningful text privatization happens on complete documents. Recent research has laid the groundwork for reasoning about privacy budget distribution, namely, how an overall $\varepsilon$ budget can be sensibly distributed among the component pieces of a text. We perform a systematic evaluation of multiple text decomposition and budget distribution techniques in the context of DP text obfuscation, testing how different methods for chunking texts can be combined with techniques for allocating $\varepsilon$ to these chunks. Our experiments reveal that such design choices are very important, as even with comparable privacy budgets, significantly different results can occur based on which methods are chosen. In this, we provide credible evidence of the feasibility of maximizing empirical trade-offs by optimizing DP obfuscation procedures.

</details>


### [164] [Controlled Paraphrase Geometry in Sentence Embedding Space: Local Manifold Modeling and Latent Probing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01073)
*Leonid Bedratyuk*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究语义相似句子的嵌入云局部几何结构，提出了基于低阶拟合模型（仿射、二次、三次）的局部几何建模方案和基于曲面的潜在探针方法，在受控数据集CoPaGE-300K上实验表明非线性模型比线性模型更准确，但几何有效性并不自动转化为分类性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究受控释义类语义变体在句子嵌入空间中的局部组织方式，以及这种局部结构是否能够通过低阶拟合载波（低次多项式模型）显式建模。

Method: 提出基于仿射、二次和三次拟合模型的局部几何建模方案，以及基于曲面的潜在探针方法：在降维后的局部PCA空间中构建合成潜在点。通过表面一致性、邻域结构保持、经验分布一致性、Hessian二阶形状描述符稳定性和拟合模型系数稳定性等指标评估生成点质量。

Result: 在受控语义相似句集上，非线性局部模型比仿射模型更准确描述嵌入云；曲面生成在拟合几何保真度方面表现优秀；但下游实验显示几何有效性并不自动转化为分类性能提升。

Conclusion: 支持对句子嵌入空间进行显式局部几何建模，同时强调需要区分几何有效性和判别实用性。作为资源贡献，发布了CoPaGE-300K数据集。

Abstract: The paper studies the local geometry of embedding clouds induced by \emph{controlled local classes of semantically close sentences}. The central question is how controlled paraphrase-like semantic variation is organized in sentence embedding space and whether this local structure can be explicitly modeled by low-degree fitted carriers.
  We introduce a local geometric modeling scheme based on affine, quadratic, and cubic fitted models. We also use a surface-based latent probing procedure that constructs synthetic latent points in a reduced local PCA space with respect to the fitted carrier. The procedure is intended as an offline method for representation-space analysis, local manifold modeling, and geometry-aware latent probing.
  Generated latent points are evaluated using criteria that measure consistency with the fitted surface, preservation of neighborhood structure, agreement with the empirical distribution, stability of Hessian-based second-order shape descriptors, and stability of fitted-model coefficients. Experiments on controlled sets of semantically close sentences show that nonlinear local models describe embedding clouds more accurately than affine models. Surface-based generation provides strong fitted-geometry fidelity, including surface consistency, Hessian-based shape consistency, and coefficient consistency.
  Downstream experiments show that geometric validity of synthetic latent points does not automatically translate into improved classification performance. The results support explicit local geometric modeling of sentence embedding space and highlight the need to distinguish geometric validity from discriminative utility. As a resource contribution, we introduce \textbf{CoPaGE-300K}, a controlled template-based dataset of semantically close sentence variants with slot-level annotations and precomputed sentence embeddings.

</details>


### [165] [Teaching LLMs Brazilian Healthcare: Injecting Knowledge from Official Clinical Guidelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01077)
*Hugo Abonizio,Filipe Rocha Lopes,Roberto Lotufo,Rodrigo Nogueira*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 通过持续预训练和GRPO强化学习，将Qwen2.5-14B适配到巴西临床领域，生成约7000万token合成数据，构建了HealthBench-BR和PCDT-QA两个基准，最佳模型在14B参数下超越了GPT-5.2等更大模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 巴西统一医疗系统(SUS)依赖官方临床指南，但现有LLM对此类指南特定知识表现不佳，且缺乏基于巴西葡萄牙语协议的临床召回基准。

Method: 从178份官方临床指南(~5.4M tokens)出发，使用4个生成器LLM生成约7000万token的三种格式(重述、维基风格文章、问答对)合成数据；采用持续预训练+组相对策略优化(GRPO)；构建了包含1780个平衡真假临床断言的HealthBench-BR和890个开放式临床问题的PCDT-QA基准。

Result: 最佳模型在HealthBench-BR上达83.9%，在PCDT-QA上达85.4%，超越GPT-5.2、Claude Sonnet 4.6、Gemini 3.1 Pro以及基于网页的RAG系统，消融实验证明生成器多样性和强化学习对性能提升至关重要。

Conclusion: 展示了基于合成数据和强化学习的小规模领域适配方法可以有效超越大规模通用模型，并公开所有数据集、基准和模型权重以支持可复现的巴西葡萄牙语临床NLP研究。

Abstract: Brazil's Unified Health System (SUS) relies on official clinical guidelines that define diagnostic criteria, treatments, dosages, and monitoring procedures for over 200 million citizens. Yet current LLMs perform poorly on this guideline-specific knowledge, and no benchmark evaluates clinical recall grounded in Brazilian Portuguese protocols. We address this gap by adapting Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct to the Brazilian clinical domain. From 178 official guidelines (~5.4M tokens), we generate ~70M tokens of synthetic data in three formats -- rephrases, wiki-style articles, and question-answer pairs -- using four generator LLMs. We then apply continual pre-training followed by Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO). We introduce HealthBench-BR, with 1,780 balanced true/false clinical assertions, and PCDT-QA, with 890 open-ended clinical questions scored by an LLM judge. Our best model achieves 83.9% on HealthBench-BR and 85.4% on PCDT-QA, outperforming GPT-5.2, Claude Sonnet 4.6, Gemini 3.1 Pro, and Google AI Overview's web-grounded RAG despite having only 14B parameters. Ablations show that generator diversity and reinforcement learning are critical to these gains. We release all datasets, benchmarks, and model weights to support reproducible clinical NLP research for Brazilian Portuguese. Code, data, and model weights are available at https://github.com/hugoabonizio/clinical-protocols-br

</details>


### [166] [Interpretable Difficulty-Aware Knowledge Tracing in Tutor-Student Dialogues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01097)
*Shuyan Huang,Alexander Scarlatos,Jaewook Lee,Andrew Lan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于大语言模型的可解释难度感知对话知识追踪框架，通过显式建模学生能力和问题难度，并整合项目反应理论，在对话场景中实现准确且可解释的学生表现预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对话式知识追踪方法常忽略问题难度建模，且依赖LLM产生的不透明潜在表征，导致预测不准确且缺乏可解释性。

Method: 1) 基于LLM构建对话式知识追踪框架，显式建模每轮对话中学生能力与教师提问难度；2) 融入原始文本问题和下一轮教师提问来估计学生知识状态与下一轮任务难度；3) 整合项目反应理论(IRT)将LLM输出映射为学生能力和问题难度参数。

Result: 在两个师生对话数据集上的定量和定性结果均表明，该框架优于现有知识追踪基线方法，同时生成的输出与认知理论一致，具有可解释性。

Conclusion: 该工作首次将项目反应理论与LLM结合用于对话知识追踪，在提升预测准确性的同时保证了模型的可解释性，为智能 tutoring 系统的个性化支持提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have led to the development of AI-powered tutoring systems that provide interactive support via dialogue. To enable these tutoring systems to provide personalized support, it is essential to assess student performance at each turn, motivating knowledge tracing (KT) in dialogue settings. However, existing dialogue-based KT approaches often ignore question difficulty modeling and rely on opaque latent representations from LLMs, hindering accurate and interpretable prediction. In this work, we propose an interpretable difficulty-aware conversational KT framework built upon LLMs, which explicitly models students' abilities and the difficulty of tutor-posed tasks at each turn. The framework incorporates the original textual question and the next tutor-posed task to estimate the student's knowledge state and the difficulty of the upcoming turn. Furthermore, it integrates Item Response Theory to map LLM's outputs into student ability and question difficulty parameters, enabling interpretable prediction of student performance grounded in cognitive theories of learning. We evaluate the framework on two tutor-student dialogue datasets. Both quantitative and qualitative results show that our framework outperforms existing KT baselines, meanwhile generating interpretable outputs consistent with cognitive theory.

</details>


### [167] [Quantifying and Predicting Disagreement in Graded Human Ratings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01168)
*Leixin Zhang,Çağrı Çöltekin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究探讨了不当语言标注中的评分分歧模式，提出"对立指数"量化标注者意见对立程度，发现标注方差可被文本特征预测，且高对立指数的项目更难被模型预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类标注者之间存在固有分歧，且不同实例的分歧程度不同。作者希望通过量化标注变异模式，理解并预测哪些文本会引发标注者意见对立，从而改进对争议性内容的建模。

Method: 使用分级人类评分数据，通过文本特征预测标注方差；提出"对立指数"（Opposition Index）量化标注者间的意见对立程度；比较直接预测方差与从预测分布估计方差两种方法的性能。

Result: 估计方差与观测方差之间呈现中等正相关；直接预测方差与从预测分布估计方差两种方法性能相当；高对立指数的项目更难预测且常被模型低估。

Conclusion: 标注方差可通过文本特征预测，但存在极端意见对立（高对立指数）的项目对现有模型构成挑战，需在建模时给予特殊关注。

Abstract: It is increasingly recognized that human annotators do not always agree, and such disagreement is inherent in many annotation tasks. However, not all instances in a given task elicit the same degree of opinion divergence. In this paper, we investigate annotation variation patterns in graded human ratings for inappropriate languages, including offensive language, hate speech, and toxic language perception. We examine whether the degree of annotation disagreement can be predicted from textual features. We further propose the Opposition Index, a metric that quantifies perspective opposition among annotators on a given item, and investigate the predictability of instances with potentially opposing human opinions. Our results show a moderate positive correlation between estimated and observed annotation variance. We find that two approaches achieve comparable performance in variance prediction: directly predicting the variance value and estimating it from predicted annotation distributions. Our results on opposition perspective prediction show that items with high opposition index values are more difficult to predict and are often underestimated by models.

</details>


### [168] [Compute Optimal Tokenization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01188)
*Tomasz Limisiewicz,Artidoro Pagnoni,Srini Iyer,Mike Lewis,Sachin Mehta,Alisa Liu,Margaret Li,Gargi Ghosh,Luke Zettlemoyer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了令牌信息粒度（通过压缩率控制）对缩放定律的影响，发现计算最优配置下模型参数量与数据量（以字节计）成正比，而非以令牌计；最优压缩率随计算量增加而降低，且不同于BPE分词器得到的压缩率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缩放定律已能指导数据量和模型大小的最优选择，但数据单元（令牌）对该关系的影响尚未被充分探索。

Method: 训练了988个从50M到7B参数的潜在令牌化模型（BLT），这些模型可设置目标压缩率，从而研究压缩率（平均每令牌字节数）对缩放趋势的影响。

Result: 计算最优配置下，模型参数量与以字节计的数据量成正比，而非以令牌计；最优压缩率与BPE得到的压缩率不同，且随计算量增加而降低。

Conclusion: 这些发现适用于潜在令牌化和子词令牌化，以及英语以外的其他语言，指导语言模型开发者选择分词方案以最大化计算效率。

Abstract: Scaling laws enable the optimal selection of data amount and language model size, yet the impact of the data unit, the token, on this relationship remains underexplored. In this work, we systematically investigate how the information granularity of tokens, controlled by the compression rate (i.e., average bytes of text per token), affects scaling trends. We train 988 latent tokenized models (BLT) ranging from 50M to 7B parameters that enable setting the desired compression rate. This flexibility allows us to study the role of compression rate well beyond 4.57 bytes per token obtained with a popular BPE tokenizer. Our experiments reveal that in compute-optimal configurations, model parameter counts scale proportionally to data size measured in bytes, not in tokens as commonly perceived (Kaplan et al., 2020; Hoffmann et al., 2022). Furthermore, we discover that the optimal compression rate differs from the one obtained with BPE and decreases with compute. These findings generalize to both latent and subword tokenization, as well as to languages other than English, guiding language model developers on tokenization scheme selection for maximal compute efficiency.

</details>


### [169] [SRA: Span Representation Alignment for Large Language Model Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01205)
*Quoc Phong Dao,Hoang Son Nguyen,Pham Khanh Chi,Tung Nguyen,Linh Ngo Van,Nguyen Thi Ngoc Diep,Trung Le*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SRA提出了一种通过物理视角（多粒子动力学系统）重构跨分词器知识蒸馏的方法，将对齐单元从token转为鲁棒的、与分词器无关的span表示，利用质心（CoM）和注意力加权聚合语义信息，并引入几何正则化与跨度logit蒸馏，在跨架构蒸馏任务中显著超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CTKD方法主要聚焦于token级别的对齐策略，但这种方式对分词器之间的差异敏感且脆弱；作者认为在蒸馏前将token聚合成更鲁棒的表示同样重要，因此提出从物理多粒子动力学系统视角重新审视该问题。

Method: 将每个span建模为粒子簇，使用注意力加权的质心（CoM）表示其状态；利用注意力导出的跨度质心权重优先关注最显著的跨度；引入几何正则化保持表示空间的结构完整性；同时采用对齐的span logit蒸馏增强跨模型知识迁移。

Result: 在具有挑战性的跨架构蒸馏实验中，SRA一致且显著地优于当前最先进的CTKD基线方法。

Conclusion: SRA通过物理启发的span表示对齐方法，有效解决了CTKD中分词器差异带来的对齐困难，验证了这种物理驱动的蒸馏方式的有效性。

Abstract: Cross-Tokenizer Knowledge Distillation (CTKD) enables knowledge transfer between a large language model and a smaller student, even when they employ different tokenizers. While existing approaches mainly focus on token-level alignment strategies, which are often brittle and sensitive to discrepancies between tokenizers, we argue that the method of aggregating tokens into more robust representations before distillation is of equal importance. In this paper, we introduce \textbf{SRA} (\textbf{S}pan \textbf{R}epresentation \textbf{A}lignment for Large Language Model Distillation), a novel framework that reframes CTKD through the physical lens of Multi-Particle Dynamical Systems. SRA shifts the fundamental unit of alignment from tokens to robust, tokenizer-agnostic spans. We model each span as a cluster of particles and represent its state by its Center of Mass (CoM) - an attention-weighted average that captures rich semantic information. We leverage the concept of span centers of mass with attention-derived weighting to prioritize the most salient spans. In addition, we employ a geometric regularizer to preserve the structural integrity of the representation space and introduce aligned span logit distillation to enhance knowledge transfer across models. In challenging cross-architecture distillation experiments, SRA consistently and significantly outperforms state-of-the-art CTKD baselines, validating our physically-grounded approach.

</details>


### [170] [GIFT: Guided Fine-Tuning and Transfer for Enhancing Instruction-Tuned Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01256)
*Zhiwen Ruan,Yichao Du,Jianjie Zheng,Longyue Wang,Yun Chen,Peng Li,Jinsong Su,Yang Liu,Guanhua Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出GIFT框架，利用指令微调模型的置信度信号指导预训练基座模型上的低秩适配器微调，再将适配器合并回指令微调模型，实现任务特化与通用指令跟随能力的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将指令微调模型视为仅在最终合并阶段才参与的被动目标，未能在训练过程中利用其指导任务适应过程。

Method: GIFT框架：在预训练基座模型上微调低秩适配器，训练过程中使用来自指令微调模型的置信度信号作为指导，训练完成后将适配器合并回指令微调模型。

Result: 在数学和知识密集型基准测试中，跨多个模型家族和规模，GIFT一致优于直接微调和代表性迁移基线，同时保持鲁棒的泛化能力和良好的测试时扩展行为。

Conclusion: GIFT是一种简单高效的框架，通过引入指令模型的引导信号，有效实现了任务特化与通用指令跟随能力的兼顾。

Abstract: A promising paradigm for adapting instruction-tuned language models is to learn task-specific updates on a pretrained base model and subsequently merge them into the instruction-tuned model. However, existing approaches typically treat the instruction-tuned model as a passive target that is only involved at the final merging stage, without guiding the training process. We propose GIFT (Guided Fine-Tuning and Transfer), a simple and efficient framework that incorporates guidance from the instruction model into task adaptation. GIFT fine-tunes a low-rank adapter on the pretrained base model using confidence signals derived from the instruction-tuned model. The learned adapter is then merged into the instruction-tuned model, yielding task-specialized models that preserve general instruction-following behavior. We evaluate GIFT on mathematical and knowledge-intensive benchmarks across multiple model families and scales. Results show that GIFT consistently outperforms direct fine-tuning and representative transfer-based baselines, while maintaining robust generalization and favorable test-time scaling behavior.

</details>


### [171] [Addressing Data Scarcity in Bangla Fake News Detection: An LLM-Based Dataset Augmentation Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01292)
*Ahmed Alfey Sani,Kazi Akib Zaoad,Shefayat E Shams Adib,Md Abdul Muqtadir,Ajwad Abrar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究提出基于Gemma 3 27B IT模型的数据增强框架，通过语义过滤和随机子采样生成合成孟加拉语新闻，将少数类假新闻F1分数从0.85提升至0.88，并公开了4,545条合成样本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 孟加拉语等低资源语言的假新闻检测受限于数据规模小且类别不平衡，现有数据集虽宝贵但高度不平衡限制了模型性能，而基于大语言模型的增强方法在孟加拉语中鲜有探索。

Method: 使用指令微调的Gemma 3 27B IT模型，通过零样本和少样本提示生成合成孟加拉语新闻，结合语义过滤和受控子采样保持标签一致性，比较不同增强率和随机/基于相似度的选择策略。

Result: 仅对少数类进行高增强率+随机子采样的策略最佳，假新闻F1分数从0.85提升至0.88。

Conclusion: 精心设计的LLM驱动增强能显著改善低资源场景下的假新闻检测，为推进多语言 misinformation 研究提供了实用基础。

Abstract: The growing spread of misinformation in digital media highlights the need for reliable fake news detection systems, yet progress in under-resourced languages such as Bangla is limited by small and imbalanced datasets. This study investigates whether Large Language Model (LLM) based augmentation can effectively address this limitation and improve Bangla fake news classification. Existing datasets remain valuable but highly imbalanced, limiting model performance, and LLM based augmentation for Bangla has been scarcely explored. To fill this gap, we propose a systematic augmentation framework that generates synthetic Bangla news articles using the instruction tuned Gemma 3 27B IT model, supported by semantic filtering and controlled subsampling to preserve label consistency and diversity. We compare zero shot and few shot prompting, evaluate multiple augmentation rates, and examine random versus similarity-based selection strategies. Our experiments show that augmenting only the minority class with a high augmentation rate and random subsampling yields the strongest gains, raising the Fake News F1 score from 0.85 to 0.88. To support reproducibility and further research in this low-resource domain, we publicly release 4,545 synthetically generated Bangla fake news samples along with our full implementation. These findings demonstrate that well-designed LLM-driven augmentation can significantly improve fake news detection in low resource settings and provide a practical foundation for advancing multilingual misinformation research.

</details>


### [172] [Sentiment Analysis of Mobile Legends App Reviews Using Machine Learning and LSTM-Based Deep Learning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01317)
*Vira Putri Maharani,Kharisa Harvanny,Daris Samudra,Luluk Muthoharoh,Ardika Satria,Martin Clinton Tosima Manullang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文比较了机器学习与LSTM深度学习在Mobile Legends应用评论情感分析中的表现，LSTM以92%准确率和91%加权F1-score优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究深度学习（LSTM）是否比传统机器学习方法（TF-IDF+PyCaret AutoML）更有效地处理非正式、上下文依赖的用户评论情感分析任务。

Method: 使用10,000条标注为正面、负面和中性评论的数据集，对比传统机器学习（TF-IDF+PyCaret AutoML）与LSTM深度学习模型在情感分析上的性能。

Result: LSTM模型以92%准确率和91%加权F1-score显著优于传统机器学习基线模型。

Conclusion: 深度学习方法在处理非正式且依赖上下文的用户评论文本方面比传统机器学习更具有效性。

Abstract: This paper compares Machine Learning and LSTM-based Deep Learning methods for sentiment analysis of Mobile Legends app reviews. Using a dataset of 10,000 reviews labeled as positive, negative, and neutral, the study evaluates traditional models with TF-IDF and PyCaret AutoML and compares them against an LSTM model designed to capture sequential text dependencies. The results show that the LSTM model outperforms the classical Machine Learning baselines, achieving 92% accuracy and a weighted F1-score of 91%. The findings indicate that deep learning is more effective for handling informal and context-dependent user review text.

</details>


### [173] [Benchmarking LightGBM and BiLSTM for Sentiment Analysis on Indonesian E-Commerce Reviews](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01322)
*Lidia Natasyah Marpaung,Vania Claresta,Iqfina Haula Halika,Luluk Muthoharoh,Ardika Satria,Martin Clinton Tosima Manullang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究比较了基于PyCaret AutoML的机器学习（LightGBM、逻辑回归、SVM）与深度学习（BiLSTM）在印尼电商评论情感分析任务中的表现。实验结果表明，BiLSTM模型以98.87%的准确率和F1分数 outperforms 所有ML模型，而LightGBM是表现最佳的ML模型（准确率98.23%），训练时间高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在印尼电商评论的情感分析任务中，系统比较传统机器学习（AutoML框架）与深度学习方法的表现差异，为印尼语NLP任务选择合适的技术方案提供实证依据。

Method: 使用Hugging Face上的15,000条印尼电商评论数据集，划分训练/验证/测试集。ML方面采用PyCaret AutoML框架比较LightGBM、逻辑回归和SVM三种算法；DL方面采用BiLSTM架构。通过准确率和F1分数评估性能。

Result: BiLSTM达到最高性能：准确率98.87%，F1-Score 98.87%。ML模型中LightGBM最优：准确率98.23%，且训练时间高效。逻辑回归和SVM表现相对较低。

Conclusion: BiLSTM架构在捕捉印尼语评论文本的序列上下文方面具有显著优势，是该特定分类任务的首选模型。同时，LightGBM作为ML模型在效率与性能间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: This study presents a comparative analysis between two primary approaches in Natural Language Processing (NLP): Machine Learning (ML) utilizing the PyCaret AutoML framework, and Deep Learning (DL). The evaluation is conducted on a sentiment analysis task using an Indonesian e-commerce review dataset sourced from Hugging Face. The dataset, consisting of 15,000 samples, is partitioned into training, validation, and testing sets. The ML experiments compare LightGBM, Logistic Regression, and Support Vector Machine (SVM) algorithms, whereas the DL experiment implements a Bidirectional Long Short-Term Memory (BiLSTM) architecture. The experimental results demonstrate that the BiLSTM model outperforms all ML models, achieving an accuracy of 98.87\% and an F1-Score of 98.87\%. Meanwhile, LightGBM emerges as the best-performing ML model with an accuracy of 98.23\% in a highly efficient training time. This research proves that the BiLSTM architecture is highly capable of capturing the sequential context of Indonesian review texts, making it the superior model for this specific classification task.

</details>


### [174] [Creating and Evaluating Figurative Language Dataset for Sindhi](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01323)
*Wazir Ali,Adeeb Noor,Saifullah Tumrani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了SiNFluD——首个信德语比喻语言分类基准数据集，通过多来源文本收集、人工标注（一致性0.81）和多种预训练模型评估，发现XLM-RoBERTa-XL表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信德语（Sindhi）在自然语言处理领域资源匮乏，尤其缺乏用于比喻语言分类的标准数据集，本研究旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 从博客、社交媒体和文学来源收集原始文本，使用Doccano工具由两位母语者进行标注，采用5折和10折交叉验证建立基线，评估mBERT、XLM-RoBERTa、XLM-RoBERTa-XL及SetFit（少样本微调）等模型。

Result: 标注者间一致性达0.81；预训练XLM-RoBERTa-XL在所有评估模型中取得最佳性能。

Conclusion: SiNFluD为信德语比喻语言分类提供了可靠的基准数据集，跨语言预训练模型（尤其是XLM-RoBERTa-XL）在该低资源语言任务上表现优越。

Abstract: In this article, we introduce SiNFluD, a novel benchmark dataset for Sindhi figurative language classification. We first collect raw text from various blogs, social media platforms, and literary sources, and subsequently prepare the corpus for annotation. Two native annotators label the data using the Doccano text annotation tool, achieving an inter-annotator agreement of 0.81. We then establish baseline results using 5-fold and 10-fold cross-validation. Finally, we evaluate mBERT, XLM-RoBERTa, and XLM-RoBERTa-XL models, along with SetFit for few-shot fine-tuning of sentence transformers. Among these, the pretrained XLM-RoBERTa-XL achieves the best performance.

</details>


### [175] [OralMLLM-Bench: Evaluating Cognitive Capabilities of Multimodal Large Language Models in Dental Practice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01333)
*Rongyang Wang,Shuang Zhou,Jiashuo Wang,Wenya Xie,Xiaoxia Che*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个评估多模态大语言模型在牙科放射影像分析中认知能力的综合基准，涵盖三种影像模态和四个认知层级（感知、理解、预测、决策），包含27项临床任务、3,820份临床评估，并测试了GPT-5.2和GLM-4.6等前沿模型，揭示了MLLMs与临床医生之间的性能差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型在牙科影像分析中展现出潜力，但其模拟放射影像分析所需的多层次认知过程的能力尚不清楚，缺乏系统性的评估基准。

Method: 构建了一个涵盖根尖片、全景片和侧位头影测量片三种关键影像模态的基准，定义了感知、理解、预测和决策四个认知类别，包含27项源自公开数据集的临床任务，并经过人工标注和3,820份临床医生评估，对GPT-5.2和GLM-4.6等六个前沿MLLMs进行评测。

Result: 展示了MLLMs与临床医生在牙科实践中的性能差距，描绘了模型的优势与局限，刻画了失败模式，并提出了改进建议。

Conclusion: 该数据资源将促进与临床认知、安全需求和牙科工作流复杂性相对齐的下一代人工智能系统的发展。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have emerged as a promising paradigm for dental image analysis. However, their ability to capture the multi-level cognitive processes required for radiographic analysis remains unclear. Here, we present a comprehensive benchmark to evaluate the cognitive capabilities of MLLMs in dental radiographic analysis. It spans three critical imaging modalities, i.e., periapical, panoramic, and lateral cephalometric radiographs, and defines four cognitive categories: perception, comprehension, prediction, and decision-making. The benchmark comprises 27 clinically grounded tasks derived from public datasets, with manually curated annotations and 3,820 clinician assessments for evaluation. Six frontier MLLMs, including GPT-5.2 and GLM-4.6, are evaluated. We demonstrate the performance gap between MLLMs and clinicians in dental practice, delineate model strengths and limitations, characterize failure patterns, and provide recommendations for improvement. This data resource will facilitate the development of next-generation artificial intelligence systems aligned with clinical cognition, safety requirements, and workflow complexity in dental practice.

</details>


### [176] [A Multi-View Media Profiling Suite: Resources, Evaluation, and Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01336)
*Muhammad Arslan Manzoor,Dilshod Azizov,Daniil Orel,Umer Siddique,Zain Muhammad Mujahid,Yufang Hou,Preslav Nakov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过构建大规模标签集MBFC-2025、多视图表示（涵盖Alexa图、超链接图、LLM衍生图、文章和维基百科描述）、系统评估嵌入视图与融合策略（含强化学习融合变体），在ACL-2020上取得SOTA结果并为MBFC-2025建立强基准，系统性填补了新闻政治偏见与事实性自动检测领域在统一资源、综合评估和表征融合策略分析方面的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前新闻政治偏见与事实性自动检测领域缺乏统一资源、缺乏对不同方法的全面评估、缺乏对表征和融合策略的系统分析（尤其在标签稀疏和数据集多样性条件下），且缺乏基于观测的实证研究来报告什么有效、什么失败及其原因。

Method: (1) 构建MBFC-2025大规模标签集（约2600个新闻机构）；(2) 为ACL-2020（约900个机构）和MBFC-2025构建多视图表示，包括Alexa图、超链接图、LLM衍生图、文章和维基百科描述；(3) 系统评估嵌入视图和融合策略，包括基于强化学习的融合变体；(4) 进行大量实验。

Result: 在ACL-2020数据集上实现了最先进（SOTA）的结果，并为MBFC-2025建立了强基准。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了领域内统一资源、综合评估和系统分析方面的空白，为后续研究提供了大规模标签集和基准。

Abstract: News outlets shape public opinion at a scale that makes automated detection of political bias and factuality essential. However, the field still lacks unified resources, comprehensive evaluations across diverse approaches, and systematic analyses of the representations and fusion strategies that matter most, especially under label sparsity and dataset diversity. In addition, there is little empirical work reporting broad, observation-driven findings about what consistently works, what fails, and why. We address these gaps through four main contributions. First, we introduce MBFC-2025, a large-scale label set covering approximately 2,600 outlets from Media Bias/Fact Check (MBFC). Second, we construct multiview representations for ACL-2020 (Panayotov et al., 2022), which includes around 900 outlets, as well as for MBFC-2025. These representations span Alexa graphs, hyperlink graphs, LLM-derived graphs, articles, and Wikipedia descriptions. Third, we provide a systematic evaluation and analysis of embedding views and fusion strategies, including a reinforcement learning-based fusion variant. Fourth, we conduct extensive experiments that achieve state-of-the-art results on ACL-2020 and establish strong benchmarks on MBFC-2025.

</details>


### [177] [LLM Output Detectability and Task Performance Can be Jointly Optimized](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01350)
*Koshiro Saito,Ryuto Koike,Masahiro Kaneko,Naoaki Okazaki*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PUPPET是一种通过强化学习微调LLM的框架，使生成的文本在保持高可检测性的同时提升下游任务性能，仅需少量样本和少量GPU时间即可实现，且跨领域、模型族和规模表现一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有水印方法虽能可靠检测机器生成文本，但会降低LLM在下游任务上的性能表现，需要在可检测性和任务性能之间寻找平衡。

Method: 采用强化学习微调LLM，设计两个奖励函数：一个输出机器类别概率的检测器和一个衡量任务特定指标的评估器，联合优化以兼顾可检测性和任务性能。

Result: 在长文本问答、摘要和作文写作任务上，PUPPET训练后的LLM在可检测性上与水印方法相当，但下游任务表现更优；仅需数千样本和1-2 GPU小时即可高效完成优化；效果在跨领域、不同LLM家族和模型规模上一致，且对释义攻击具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: PUPPET有效解决了水印方法导致下游性能下降的问题，为LLM部署中的可检测性与任务性能兼顾提供了可行方案，且具有高效、泛化性强和鲁棒等特点。

Abstract: Detecting machine-generated text is essential for transparency and accountability when deploying large language models (LLMs). Among detection approaches, watermarking is a statistically reliable method by design -- it embeds detectable signals into LLM outputs by biasing their token distributions. However, it has been reported that watermarked LLMs often perform worse on downstream tasks. We propose PUPPET, a framework that fine-tunes an LLM via reinforcement learning to generate text that is both more detectable and better performing on downstream tasks. We use two reward functions: a detector that outputs a machine-class likelihood and an evaluator that measures a task-specific metric. Experiments on long-form QA, summarization, and essay writing show that LLMs trained with PUPPET achieve high detectability competitive with watermarking methods while outperforming them on downstream tasks. The analysis shows that this optimization can be performed efficiently with only a few thousand samples in 1--2 GPU hours. Moreover, these gains are consistent across out-of-domain tasks, different LLM families, and model sizes, and are even robust to paraphrasing attacks.

</details>


### [178] [On Stable Long-Form Generation: Benchmarking and Mitigating Length Volatility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01357)
*Zhitao He,Haolin Yang,Rui Min,Zeyu Qin,Yi R. Fung*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对LLM在长文本生成中的输出长度波动问题，提出了VOLTBench基准、分析了波动内部原因，并设计了GLoBo方法（无需训练的轻量级解码优化），使平均生成长度提升148%、长度波动降低69%，同时保持高质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注单次长文本生成质量，普遍忽略了输出的波动性（volatility），这种波动不仅导致显著的计算开销，还严重影响模型在实际应用中的可靠性。

Method: 研究分为三步：1) 提出VOLTBench——一个异构任务基准，系统量化长文本生成的长度波动；2) 通过分析注意力轨迹（attention traces），深入探测导致波动的常见内部模式；3) 提出GLoBo（Stable Generation via Logits Boosting）——一种轻量级解码阶段优化策略，无需额外训练即可提升生成长度的准确性与稳定性。

Result: 在VOLTBench上的大量实验首次系统确认了主流模型存在严重的输出长度不稳定问题；GLoBo方法使基础模型平均输出长度提升148%，长度波动降低69%，同时保持高生成质量。

Conclusion: 长文本生成的长度波动是一个普遍且严重的问题；通过注意力分析可以识别其内部模式；GLoBo方法能有效缓解该问题，为实际应用提供了轻量级解决方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) excel at long-context understanding but exhibit significant limitations in long-form generation. Existing studies primarily focus on single-generation quality, generally overlooking the volatility of the output. This volatility not only leads to significant computational costs but also severely impacts the models' reliable application. To address this gap, our work unfolds in three stages: benchmarking, probing, and mitigation. We first propose the VOlatility in Long-form Text Benchmark (VOLTBench), a novel heterogeneous-task benchmark designed to systematically quantify the length volatility of long-form generation. Subsequently, by analyzing attention traces, we conduct an in-depth probe to identify several common internal patterns that cause this volatility. Finally, to mitigate long-form output volatility, we propose Stable Generation via Logits Boosting (GLoBo), a lightweight decoding-stage optimization strategy, designed to significantly enhance both the length accuracy and stability of long-form generation without additional training. Extensive experiments on VOLTBench provide the first systematic confirmation of severe long-form output instability in mainstream models and validate that our proposed method successfully improves the mean output length of the base model by 148% and reduces the length volatility by 69%, while maintaining high generation quality.

</details>


### [179] [Embedding-based In-Context Prompt Training for Enhancing LLMs as Text Encoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01372)
*Ailiang Lin,Zhuoyun Li,Keyu Mao,Kotaro Funakoshi,Manabu Okumura*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出EPIC方法，用连续嵌入替代离散文本演示作为上下文示例，在提升LLM嵌入质量的同时减少计算开销，在MTEB基准上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ICL通过预置任务相关演示能增强LLM的表示能力，但增加了序列长度导致大量token开销，需要一种既能利用ICL优势又能降低计算负担的方法。

Method: 提出EPIC（Embedding-based In-Context Prompt Training），用连续嵌入替换离散文本演示作为上下文提示，在对比学习中促使模型对齐语义相关文本对，并要求模型将演示嵌入作为上下文提示的一部分进行理解。

Result: 在MTEB基准上达到新SOTA，超越仅使用公开检索数据训练的先进模型；消融实验验证了方法的有效性和必要性。

Conclusion: EPIC训练的模型在推理时无论是否使用上下文提示都能保持优秀的嵌入性能，有效解决了ICL的token开销问题。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have been widely explored for embedding generation. While recent studies show that in-context learning (ICL) effectively enhances the representational capability of LLMs by prepending a few task-related demonstrations, it causes substantial token overhead due to the increased sequence length. In this work, we propose EPIC, a novel embedding-based in-context prompt training strategy that leverages ICL to generate high-quality embeddings while reducing computational burden during both training and inference. This approach replaces discrete text demonstrations with their corresponding continuous embeddings, which not only encourages the LLM to align semantically-related text pairs during contrastive learning, but also requires the model to interpret demonstration embeddings as part of the in-context prompt. Consequently, EPIC-trained models achieve excellent embedding performance both with or without in-context prompts at inference time. Comprehensive experiments demonstrate that our method establishes new state-of-the-art results on the MTEB benchmark, surpassing frontier models trained solely on publicly available retrieval data. Extensive ablation studies further validate the effectiveness and necessity of our mechanism.

</details>


### [180] [MTA: Multi-Granular Trajectory Alignment for Large Language Model Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01374)
*Pham Khanh Chi,Quoc Phong Dao,Thuat Nguyen,Linh Ngo Van,Trung Le,Thanh Hong Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出多粒度轨迹对齐（MTA）框架，通过按层自适应地对齐教师与学生模型在词级和短语级的表征演化轨迹，提升知识蒸馏效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识蒸馏方法在固定层或token级对齐表征，忽略了表征随深度演化的过程，导致学生模型难以捕捉教师内部的关系结构，限制了知识迁移效果。

Method: 提出MTA框架，包含：(1) 层自适应策略——低层词级对齐保留词汇信息，高层短语级对齐捕获组合语义；(2) 动态结构对齐损失——匹配各层内语义单元的相对几何关系；(3) 隐藏表征对齐损失——直接对齐选定的师生层。

Result: 在标准基准测试上，MTA持续优于现有最先进基线方法，消融实验验证了各组件的贡献。

Conclusion: 通过沿层间变换轨迹对齐表征，并引入不同粒度的语义单元匹配，能有效提升知识蒸馏中学生模型对教师内部结构的学习效果。

Abstract: Knowledge distillation is a key technique for compressing large language models (LLMs), but most existing methods align representations at fixed layers or token-level outputs, ignoring how representations evolve across depth. As a result, the student is only weakly guided to capture the teacher's internal relational structure during distillation, which limits knowledge transfer. To address this limitation, we propose Multi-Granular Trajectory Alignment (MTA), a framework that aligns teacher and student representations along their layer-wise transformation trajectory. MTA adopts a layer-adaptive strategy: lower layers are aligned at the word level to preserve lexical information, while higher layers operate on phrase-level spans (e.g., noun and verb phrases) to capture compositional semantics. We instantiate this idea through a Dynamic Structural Alignment loss that matches the relative geometry among semantic units within each layer. This design is motivated by empirical findings that Transformer representations become increasingly abstract with depth, and is also consistent with linguistic views in which higher-level meaning emerges through the composition of lower-level lexical units. We further incorporate a Hidden Representation Alignment loss to directly align selected teacher-student layers. Experiments show that MTA consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines on standard benchmarks, with ablations confirming the contribution of each component.

</details>


### [181] [A framework for analyzing concept representations in neural models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01381)
*Burin Naowarat,Hao Tang,Sharon Goldwater*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一框架，沿“包含性”（概念是否完全且仅存在于某子空间）和“解缠性”（是否与其他概念隔离）两个维度分析神经表示中的概念子空间。实验发现：概念子空间不唯一；不同估计方法影响两个维度；LEACE方法表现良好但泛化能力有限；HuBERT语音表示中，音素信息既包含又解缠，而说话人信息虽解缠但难以紧凑包含。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解神经模型如何表示人类可解释的概念具有挑战性。先前工作从探测和概念擦除等不同角度探索了线性概念子空间，但缺乏统一的比较框架，因此需要系统性地研究概念子空间的包含性与解缠性。

Method: 提出一个统一框架，沿“包含性”（概念是否完全被表示在子空间内且不在其外）和“解缠性”（是否与其他概念隔离）两个轴分析概念子空间。在文本和语音模型上，比较了五种不同领域提出的概念子空间估计方法。

Result: (1) 不同估计方法显著影响包含性和解缠性；(2) LEACE在两项指标上表现良好，但在未见数据上泛化能力不足；(3) HuBERT语音表示中，音素信息既包含又解缠，而说话人信息虽与音素解缠，但难以被紧凑地包含在一个子空间中。

Conclusion: 概念子空间并非唯一确定，这对概念子空间分析具有重要意义；不同估计方法的选择会显著影响子空间性质；需要进一步改进方法以提升泛化能力。

Abstract: Understanding how neural models represent human-interpretable concepts is challenging. Prior work has explored linear concept subspaces from diverse perspectives, such as probing and concept erasure. We introduce a unified framework to study these subspaces along two axes: \textit{containment}, which tests if a concept is fully represented in a subspace but not outside it, and \textit{disentanglement}, which tests for isolation from other concepts. In experiments on both text and speech models, we first highlight that concept subspaces may not be uniquely determined, and discuss the implications for concept subspace analysis. Then, we compare properties of concept subspaces estimated using five estimators, proposed in different communities. We find that (1) the choice of estimator impacts the containment and disentanglement properties; (2) the state-of-the-art concept erasure method, LEACE, performs well on both testing axes, but still struggles to generalize to unseen data; and (3) in HuBERT speech representations, phone information is both contained and disentangled from speaker information, while speaker information is hard to contain in a compact subspace, despite being disentangled from phones.

</details>


### [182] [MemORAI: Memory Organization and Retrieval via Adaptive Graph Intelligence for LLM Conversational Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01386)
*Hung Pham Van,Nguyen Manh Hieu,Khang Pham Tran Tuan,Nam Le Hai,Linh Ngo Van,Nguyen Thi Ngoc Diep,Trung Le*
